Surah: 75. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s075/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 75

- 75:1 لَآ أُقْسِمُ بِيَوْمِ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ
- 75:2 وَلَآ أُقْسِمُ بِٱلنَّفْسِ ٱللَّوَّامَةِ
- 75:3 أَيَحْسَبُ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ أَلَّن نَّجْمَعَ عِظَامَهُۥ
- 75:4 بَلَىٰ قَٰدِرِينَ عَلَىٰٓ أَن نُّسَوِّىَ بَنَانَهُۥ
- 75:5 بَلْ يُرِيدُ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ لِيَفْجُرَ أَمَامَهُۥ
- 75:6 يَسْـَٔلُ أَيَّانَ يَوْمُ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ
- 75:7 فَإِذَا بَرِقَ ٱلْبَصَرُ
- 75:8 وَخَسَفَ ٱلْقَمَرُ
- 75:9 وَجُمِعَ ٱلشَّمْسُ وَٱلْقَمَرُ
- 75:10 يَقُولُ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ يَوْمَئِذٍ أَيْنَ ٱلْمَفَرُّ
- 75:11 كَلَّا لَا وَزَرَ
- 75:12 إِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ يَوْمَئِذٍ ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ
- 75:13 يُنَبَّؤُا۟ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ يَوْمَئِذٍۭ بِمَا قَدَّمَ وَأَخَّرَ
- 75:14 بَلِ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ عَلَىٰ نَفْسِهِۦ بَصِيرَةٌۭ
- 75:15 وَلَوْ أَلْقَىٰ مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ
- 75:16 لَا تُحَرِّكْ بِهِۦ لِسَانَكَ لِتَعْجَلَ بِهِۦٓ
- 75:17 إِنَّ عَلَيْنَا جَمْعَهُۥ وَقُرْءَانَهُۥ
- 75:18 فَإِذَا قَرَأْنَٰهُ فَٱتَّبِعْ قُرْءَانَهُۥ
- 75:19 ثُمَّ إِنَّ عَلَيْنَا بَيَانَهُۥ
- 75:20 كَلَّا بَلْ تُحِبُّونَ ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ
- 75:21 وَتَذَرُونَ ٱلْءَاخِرَةَ
- 75:22 وُجُوهٌۭ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ نَّاضِرَةٌ
- 75:23 إِلَىٰ رَبِّهَا نَاظِرَةٌۭ
- 75:24 وَوُجُوهٌۭ يَوْمَئِذٍۭ بَاسِرَةٌۭ
- 75:25 تَظُنُّ أَن يُفْعَلَ بِهَا فَاقِرَةٌۭ
- 75:26 كَلَّآ إِذَا بَلَغَتِ ٱلتَّرَاقِىَ
- 75:27 وَقِيلَ مَنْ ۜ رَاقٍۢ
- 75:28 وَظَنَّ أَنَّهُ ٱلْفِرَاقُ
- 75:29 وَٱلْتَفَّتِ ٱلسَّاقُ بِٱلسَّاقِ
- 75:30 إِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ يَوْمَئِذٍ ٱلْمَسَاقُ
- 75:31 فَلَا صَدَّقَ وَلَا صَلَّىٰ
- 75:32 وَلَٰكِن كَذَّبَ وَتَوَلَّىٰ
- 75:33 ثُمَّ ذَهَبَ إِلَىٰٓ أَهْلِهِۦ يَتَمَطَّىٰٓ
- 75:34 أَوْلَىٰ لَكَ فَأَوْلَىٰ
- 75:35 ثُمَّ أَوْلَىٰ لَكَ فَأَوْلَىٰٓ
- 75:36 أَيَحْسَبُ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ أَن يُتْرَكَ سُدًى
- 75:37 أَلَمْ يَكُ نُطْفَةًۭ مِّن مَّنِىٍّۢ يُمْنَىٰ
- 75:38 ثُمَّ كَانَ عَلَقَةًۭ فَخَلَقَ فَسَوَّىٰ
- 75:39 فَجَعَلَ مِنْهُ ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ ٱلذَّكَرَ وَٱلْأُنثَىٰٓ
- 75:40 أَلَيْسَ ذَٰلِكَ بِقَٰدِرٍ عَلَىٰٓ أَن يُحْۦِىَ ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s075/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ب ر ق (root_000108): 75:7 بَرِقَ

- **B001** parlamak veya parlaklığını göstermek — şimşek parıltısı · gökte şimşek çaktı · parıldayan; şimşekli bulut · parlaklık, parıltı · kılıcını parıldatarak gösterdi · yemeği az yağla parlattı · süslenip kendini gösterdi
  أحدهما لمعان الشيء؛ البرق وميض السحاب؛ كل شيء يتلألأ لونه فهو بارق؛ للسيوف بوارق؛ برقت السماء إذا جاءت ببرق؛ برق وجهه بالدهن؛ برق طعامه بالزيت أو السمن (maqayis)؛ والبروق بيض السحاب؛ كل شيء يتلألا فهو بارق؛ يقال للسيوف بوارق (ayn)؛ برق السيف وغيره يبرق بروقا أي تلألأ؛ والبرق واحد بروق السحاب؛ أبرق الرجل إذا لمع بسيفه؛ برقوا لنا طعاما بزيت أو سمن (sihah)؛ برقت السماء ورعدت؛ أبرق الرجل بسيفه إذا لمع به؛ يقال للسلاح إذا رأيت بريقه رأيت البارقة؛ أبرقت المرأة وبرقت إذا تحسنت وتعرضت (tahdhib)؛ البرق لمعان السحاب؛ برق يقال في كل ما يلمع نحو سيف بارق؛ برق طعامه بزيت (mufradat)؛ الهبرقي الحداد أو الصائغ حتى يبرق (maqayis)

  إذا بقى كالمتحير قيل برق بصره فهو برق فزع مبهوت؛ من قرأ برق البصر فإنه يقول تراه يلمع من شدة شخوصه؛ برق بعينه إذا لألأ من شدة النظر (maqayis)؛ برق بصره فهو برق أي بهت فهو فزع مبهوت؛ تراه يلمع من شدة شخوصه ولا يطرف؛ برق بعينه تبريقا إذا لألأها من شدة النظر (ayn)؛ برق البصر بالكسر إذا تحير فلا يطرف؛ برق البصر بالفتح بريقه إذا شخص؛ برق عينيه تبريقا أوسعهما وأحد النظر (sihah)؛ برق بكسر الراء فمعناه فزع؛ برق بفتح الراء من البريق أي شخص؛ فتح عينيه من الفزع؛ برق فلان بعينيه تبريقا إذا لألأ بهما من شدة النظر؛ البرق الدهش (tahdhib)؛ برق يقال في العين إذا اضطربت وجالت من خوف (mufradat)

  إذا شدد موعد بالوعيد قيل أبرق وأرعد؛ يقال برق ورعد أيضا؛ لتبرق وترعد يعني التهدد (maqayis)؛ إذا اشتد موعد بالوعيد يقال أبرق وأرعد؛ وبرق ورعد لغة (ayn)؛ رعد الرجل وبرق أي تهدد؛ رعدت المرأة وبرقت أي تزينت (sihah)؛ برق الرجل يبرق ورعد يرعد إذا تهدد؛ أبرق وأرعد أي تهدد (tahdhib)؛ برق فلان ورعد وأبرق وأرعد إذا تهدد (mufradat)
- **B004** gerçek karşılığı olmayan belirti vermek — yağmur getirmeyen şimşek · dişi deve gebe olmadığı halde gebelik belirtisi gösterdi · gebe olmadığı halde kuyruğuyla gebelik belirtisi veren dişi deve · gerçek dayanağı olmayan bir şey ortaya attı
  أبرقت الناقة إذا ضربت ذنبها مرة على فرجها ومرة على عجزها فهي بروق ومبرق؛ برق الرجل إذا أتى بشيء لا مصداق له؛ برقت وعرقت أي لوحت بشيء ليس له حقيقة (maqayis)؛ ابرقت الناقة ضربت بذنبها مرة على فرجها ومرة على عجزها (ayn)؛ برق الخلب وهو الذي ليس فيه مطر؛ أبرقت الناقة وبرقت إذا شالت بذنبها وتلقحت وليست بلاقح (sihah)؛ أبرقت الناقة وليست بلاقح؛ ناقة بروق إذا شالت بذنبها؛ برقت أي لوحت بشيء ليس له مصداق (tahdhib)؛ ناقة بروق تلمع بذنبها (mufradat)

  الأصل الآخر اجتماع السواد والبياض في الشيء؛ تسمى العين برقاء لسوادها وبياضها؛ البرق مصدر الأبرق من الحبال والجبال؛ البرقاء من الأرض طرائق بقعة فيها حجارة سود تخالطها رملة بيضاء؛ البرقان ما اصفر من الجراد وتلونت فيه خطوط واسود؛ البرقاء من الغنم كالبلقاء من الخيل (maqayis)؛ البرق مصدر الأبرق من الحبال؛ الحبل الذي أبرم بقوة سوداء وقوة بيضاء؛ البرقاء من الأرض طرائق بقعة فيها حجارة سود يخالطها رملة بيضاء؛ البرقانة جرادة تلونت بخطوط صفر وسود (ayn)؛ الأبرق غلظ فيه حجارة ورمل وطين مختلطة؛ كل شيء اجتمع فيه سواد وبياض فهو أبرق؛ العين برقاء (sihah)؛ حبل أبرق لسواد فيه وبياض؛ كل شيئين خلطا من لونين فقد برقا؛ العين برقاء لسواد الحدقة مع بياض الشحمة؛ الجراد إذا كان فيه بياض وسواد برقان (tahdhib)؛ البرقة للأرض ذات حجارة مختلفة الألوان؛ الأبرق الجبل فيه سواد وبياض؛ سموا العين برقاء لذلك (mufradat)

  يقال للسيف ولكل ما له بريق إبريق؛ والإبريق معروف وهو من الباب (maqayis)؛ والأباريق جمع إبريق (ayn)؛ والإبريق واحد الأباريق فارسي معرب؛ والإبريق أيضا السيف الشديد البريق (sihah)؛ الإبريق إناء وجمعه أباريق؛ الإبريق السيف ها هنا سمي به لبريقه (tahdhib)؛ والبارقة والأبيرق السيف للمعانه؛ والإبريق معروف (mufradat)
- **B007** kalın ipekli kumaş — kalın ipekli kumaş

  أحدهما العلم بالشيء وبصرت بالشيء إذا صرت به بصيرا عالما والبصيرة البرهان (maqayis)؛ البصر نفاذ في القلب والبصيرة اسم لما اعتقد في القلب من الدين وحقيق الأمر واستبصر في أمره ودينه (ayn;tahdhib)؛ حسن البصيرة إذا كان مستبصرا في دينه (jamhara)؛ البصر العلم وبصرت بالشيء علمته والتبصر التأمل والتعرف والبصيرة الحجة والاستبصار في الشيء (sihah)؛ لقوة القلب المدركة بصيرة وبصر وعلى معرفة وتحقق (mufradat)

  البصرة الحجارة الرخوة وبصر بكسر الباء من الأصل الثاني (maqayis)؛ البصرة أرض حجارتها جص والبصرة الحجارة التي فيها بعض اللين (ayn)؛ البصرة حجارة رخوة وبه سميت البصرة (jamhara)؛ البصرة حجارة رخوة إلى البياض وبصر بالكسر (sihah)؛ البصر والبصرة الحجارة البراقة وأرض كأنها جبل من جص وبصر الأرض غلظها وبصر فلان تبصيرا إذا أتى البصرة (tahdhib)؛ البصرة حجارة رخوة تلمع ويقال له بصر (mufradat)

- **B008** omurgayı kıracak denli ağır felaket — omurgayı kıracak denli ağır felaket · başına belini büken bir felaket geldi · yıkıcı felaket onu ağır biçimde etkiledi
  فقرتهم الفاقرة وهي الداهية (maqayis)؛ الفاقرة الداهية تكسر فقار الظهر (ayn)؛ الفاقرة الداهية (sihah)؛ الفاقرة داهية تكسر الظهر (tahdhib)؛ فقرته فاقرة أي داهية تكسر الفقار (mufradat)

## ب ل غ (root_000151): 75:26 بَلَغَتِ

- **B001** bir yere, şeye veya son sınıra ulaşma; bağlama göre yaklaşma ya da olgunluğa erme — yere veya şeye ulaşmak · yer, zaman veya iş bakımından en son sınıra varma · belirlenmiş sürenin sonuna yaklaşmak · çocuğun olgunluk çağına erişmesi · güç veya yaş bakımından belirlenmiş sınıra erişmek
  الوصول إلى الشيء؛ بلغت المكان إذا وصلت إليه (maqayis;sihah)؛ بلغت المكان بلوغا وصلت إليه (sihah)؛ بلغ الشيء يبلغ بلوغا (ayn)؛ البلوغ والبلاغ الانتهاء إلى أقصى المقصد والمنتهى مكانا كان أو زمانا أو أمرا (mufradat)؛ المشارفة بلوغا بحق المقاربة (maqayis)؛ بلغ الغلام أدرك (sihah)
- **B002** bir şeyi, özellikle iletiyi, hedefine ulaştırma — ulaştırmak veya erişmesini sağlamak · iletiyi yerine ulaştırmak · iletme ve ulaştırma işi
  أبلغته إبلاغا؛ بلغته تبليغا في الرسالة ونحوها (ayn)؛ بلغت الرسالة تبليغا (jamhara)؛ الإبلاغ الإيصال وكذلك التبليغ؛ بلغت الرسالة (sihah)
- **B003** yaşamı sürdürmeye yetecek ölçü ve geçim aracı — yeterli miktar veya yeten şey · yaşamı sürdürecek azık ve geçimlik · eldeki şeyle yetinmek · bir iş için yeterli olma
  البلغة ما يتبلغ به من عيش؛ لي في هذا بلاغ أي كفاية (maqayis)؛ في كذا بلاغ وتبليغ أي كفاية (ayn)؛ البلغة القوت يتبلغ به الإنسان (jamhara)؛ البلاغ أيضا الكفاية؛ البلغة ما يتبلغ به من العيش؛ تبلغ بكذا أي اكتفى به (sihah)
- **B004** amacını açık ve etkili sözle anlatma yetkinliği — amacını açık ve etkili sözle anlatma yetkinliği · dili güçlü, sözünü açık ve etkili anlatan kişi · anlatılmak isteneni açık ve etkili biçimde ileten söz
  البلاغة التي يمدح بها الفصيح اللسان لأنه يبلغ بها ما يريده (maqayis)؛ رجل بلغ بليغ وقد بلغ بلاغة (ayn)؛ كلام بلغ وبليغ؛ بلغ الرجل بلاغة إذا صار بليغا (jamhara)؛ البلاغة الفصاحة؛ بلغ الرجل أي صار بليغا (sihah)
- **B005** bir şeyi ulaşılabilir en ileri dereceye götürme — iyi veya nitelikli şey · bütün gücünü kullanıp eksik bırakmamak · eksiksiz buyruk veya en güçlü biçimde pekiştirilmiş ant
  شيء بالغ أي جيد؛ المبالغة أن تبلغ من العمل جهدك (ayn)؛ شيء بالغ أي جيد؛ بلغ في الجودة مبلغا؛ أمر الله بلغ أي بالغ؛ بالغ فلان في أمري إذا لم يقصر فيه (sihah)؛ أيمان علينا بالغة أي منتهية في التوكيد (mufradat)
- **B006** düşüncesizliğine karşın istediğine ulaşan kişi — düşüncesizliğine karşın istediğini elde eden kişi
  هو أحمق بلغ وبلغ أي إنه مع حماقته يبلغ ما يريده (maqayis)؛ أحمق بلغ أي أحمق يبلغ ما يريد (jamhara)؛ أحمق بلغ أي هو مع حماقته يبلغ ما يريده (sihah)
- **B007** atı hızlandırmak için dizgini ileri verme [kalıp] — binicinin atı hızlandırmak için dizgini ileri vermesi
  بلغ الفارس يراد به أنه يمد يده بعنان فرسه ليزيد في عدوه (maqayis)؛ بلغ الفارس إذا مد يده بعنان فرسه ليزيد في جريه (sihah)
- **B008** yokluk veya hastalığın kişiyi iyice sıkıştırması [kalıp] — yokluğun veya hastalığın kişinin üzerinde ağırlaşması
  تبلغت القلة بفلان إذا اشتدت (maqayis)؛ تبلغت به العلة أي اشتدت (sihah)
- **B009** üzücü olayı duyup ondan uzak kalmayı dileme — insanı üzen ve kendisine ulaşan haber · duyalım ama başımıza gelmesin dileği

- **B010** birini kötüleyici bildirimler — bir kimseyi kötüleyen bildirimler ve çekiştirmeler

- **B011** başa gelen ağır ve yıkıcı büyük olay — ağır ve yıkıcı büyük olay · başımıza ağır ve yıkıcı bir olay geldi

## ت ر ق (root_002011): 75:26 ٱلتَّرَاقِىَ

## ر ق ي (root_000588): 75:27 رَاقٍ

- **B001** yukarı çıkmak ve aşama aşama ilerlemek — merdiven, basamak, dağ veya gökyüzüne doğru yükselmek · yukarı çıkmak, yükselmek · bir işte veya bilgide aşama aşama ilerlemek · çıkılan basamak veya yükselme yeri
  رقيت في السلم أرقى رقيا (maqayis)؛ صعد وارتقى، والمرقاة الواحدة من المراقي في الجبل والدرجة (ayn)؛ رقيت في السلم إذا صعدت، وارتقيت مثله، وترقى في العلم (sihah)؛ رقي فلان في الجبل، ارتقى يرتقي، ما زال فلان يترقى به الأمر حتى بلغ غايته (tahdhib)؛ رقيت في الدرج والسلم، ارتقيت أيضا (mufradat-588)
- **B002** Gücün yettiğince ilerle, sınırını bil [kalıp] — Gücün yettiğince ilerle ve kendi sınırını gözet
  ارق على ظلعك أي اصعد بقدر ما تطيق (maqayis)؛ ارق على ظلعك أي امش واصعد بقدر ما تطيق ولا تحمل على نفسك ما لا تطيقه (sihah)؛ ارق على ظلعك، أي الزمه واربع عليه، أي اسكت على ما فيك من العيب (tahdhib)؛ ارق على ظلعك أي اصعد وإن كنت ظالعا (mufradat-588)
- **B003** koruyucu sözler okuyup üflemek — bir kişiye koruyucu sözler okuyup üflemek · korunma veya iyileşme amacıyla okunan sözler · koruyucu sözler okuyup üfleyen kişi · kendisi veya başkası için koruyucu okuma istemek · koruyucu okumanın yöneldiği veya bunu isteyen kişi
  رقيت الإنسان من الرقية (maqayis)؛ رقى الراقي يرقي رقية ورقيا إذا عوذ ونقث في عوذته، وصاحبه رقاء وراق (ayn)؛ الرقية معروفة، استرقيته فرقانى رقية فهو راق (sihah)؛ رقى الراقي رقية ورقيا إذا عوذ ونفث في عوذته، والمرقي يسترقي (tahdhib)؛ رقيت من الرقية، وقيل من راق أي من يرقيه (mufradat-588)
- **B004** küçük kum veya toprak tümseği — vadi yanında veya kıyısında bulunan küçük kum ya da toprak tümseği · vadi yanındaki küçük kum tümseğinin öteki ad biçimi
  الرقوة فويق الدعص من الرمل، ويقال رقو بلا هاء، وأكثر ما يكون إلى جانب واد (maqayis)؛ الرقوة دعص من رمل (sihah)؛ الرقوة فويق الدعص من الرمل، وأكثر ما يكون الرقو إلى جنب الأودية، والرقوة القمزة من التراب تجتمع على شفير الوادي (tahdhib)
- **B005** üst göğüsteki boğaz önü bölümü — üst göğüsteki boğaz önü, nefesin yükseldiği bölge
  والترقوة مقدم الحلق في أعلى الصدر حيث ما يترقى فيه النفس؛ كلا إذا بلغت التراقي (mufradat-588)
- **B006** kürek kemiği eklemindeki beyaz yağ parçası — kürek kemiği eklemindeki beyaz yağ parçası
  الرقي هي الشحمة البيضاء النقية تكون في مرجع الكتف وعليها أخرى مثلها يقال لها المأنات؛ حسبتني الرقي عليها المأنات (tahdhib)

## ف ر ق (root_001148): 75:28 ٱلْفِرَاقُ

- **B001** ayırt edip birbirinden ayırma — iki şeyi birbirinden ayırıp ayırt etmek · kişilerin birbirinden ayrılması ve uzaklaşması · konunun açıklığa kavuşması · metni sağlamlaştırıp hükümlerini açıklamak ve bölümlendirmek
  أصيل صحيح يدل على تمييز وتزييل بين شيئين (maqayis)؛ الفرق تفريق بين شيئين حتى يفترقا ويتفرقا (ayn)؛ فرقت بين الشيئين أفرق فرقا وفرقانا (sihah)؛ فرقت أفرق بين الكلام وفرقت بين الأجسام (tahdhib)؛ فرقت بين الشيئين فصلت بينهما سواء كان ذلك بفصل يدركه البصر أو بفصل تدركه البصيرة (mufradat)؛ الفراق والمفارقة تكون بالأبدان أكثر (mufradat)؛ فرق لي هذا الأمر إذا تبين ووضح (tahdhib)
- **B002** parçalara ayırıp dağıtma — parçalara ayırma ve dağıtma · ayrı zamanlarda bölümler halinde ulaştırmak · sürüyü dağıtan kokarca
  أخذت حقي منه بالتفاريق (sihah)؛ قرآنا فرقناه من شدد قال أنزلناه مفرقا في أيام (sihah)؛ نزل متفرقا (tahdhib)؛ والتفريق أصله للتكثير ويقال ذلك في تشتيت الشمل والكلمة (mufradat)؛ يفرقون به بين المرء وزوجه (mufradat)؛ إن الذين فرقوا دينهم وقرئ فارقوا (mufradat)؛ ومفرق النعم هو الظربان لأنه إذا فسا بينها وهي مجتمعة تفرقت (sihah)
- **B003** doğruyu yanlıştan ayıran ölçüt veya araç — doğruyu yanlıştan ayıran kitap, kanıt, aydınlık veya destek · doğru ile yanlışın ayrıldığı belirleyici gün · doğruyu yanlıştan hakça ayıran kişi
  الفرقان كتاب الله تعالى فرق به بين الحق والباطل (maqayis)؛ الفرقان كل كتاب أنزل به فرق الله بين الحق والباطل (ayn)؛ يجعل لكم فرقانا أي حجة ظاهرة وظفرا (ayn)؛ كل ما فرق به بين الحق والباطل فهو فرقان (sihah)؛ سمى الله الكتاب المنزل على محمد فرقانا وسمى الكتاب المنزل على موسى فرقانا (tahdhib)؛ يوم الفرقان هو يوم بدر (tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الفرقان أبلغ من الفرق لأنه يستعمل في الفرق بين الحق والباطل (mufradat)؛ نورا وتوفيقا على قلوبكم يفرق به بين الحق والباطل (mufradat)
- **B004** yarılma ve ayrılan parça — yarılmış şeyden ayrılan parça veya su kütlesi · sabahın sökmesi ve aydınlığın yarılarak belirmesi
  الفرق الفلق من الشيء إذا انفلق (maqayis)؛ فانفلق فكان كل فرق كالطود العظيم (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ كل فرق كالطود العظيم يريد من الماء (ayn)؛ انفرق الصبح أي انفلق والفرق هو الفلق (ayn)؛ فانفرق البحر فصار كالجبال العظام (tahdhib)؛ الفرق الموجة والفرق الجبل والفرق الهضبة (tahdhib)؛ الفرق يقارب الفلق لكن الفلق يقال اعتبارا بالانشقاق والفرق يقال اعتبارا بالانفصال (mufradat)
- **B005** ana bütünden ayrılmış topluluk — ayrı insan topluluğu veya grup · koyun sürüsü veya sürüden ayrılan küçük koyun grubu
  الفرق القطيع من الغنم (maqayis)؛ الفريقة وهو القطيع من الغنم كأنها قطعة فارقت معظم الغنم (maqayis)؛ الفرق طائفة من الناس ومن كل شيء والفريق من الناس أكثر من الفرق (ayn)؛ الفرق بالكسر القطيع من الغنم العظيم (sihah)؛ الفرقة طائفة من الناس والفريق أكثر منهم (sihah)؛ الفريقة فريقة الغنم أن تنفرق منها قطعة أو شاة أو شاتان أو ثلاث شياه (tahdhib;sihah)؛ الفريق الجماعة المتفرقة عن آخرين (mufradat)
- **B006** ayrım çizgisi veya çatallanma noktası — saç ayrımı ve saçın ayrıldığı yer · yol ayrımı veya yolun çatallanma noktası
  من ذلك الفرق فرق الشعر (maqayis)؛ الفرق موضع المفرق من الرأس في الشعر (ayn)؛ المفرق والمفرق وسط الرأس وهو الذي يفرق فيه الشعر وكذلك مفرق الطريق ومفرقه (sihah)؛ فرق له الطريق أي اتجه له طريقان (sihah)؛ الفرق مصدر فرقت الشعر (tahdhib)؛ لا يفرق شعره إلا أن ينفرق هو (tahdhib)
- **B007** beden yapısında doğuştan ayrıklık veya eşitsizlik — beden yapısı doğuştan ayrık, aralıklı veya eşitsiz olan

  أصل يدل على مقارنة شيء لشيء (maqayis)؛ زوجان من الحمام أي ذكر وأنثى (ayn)؛ كل اثنين زوج والزوج ضد الفرد (jamhara)؛ الزوج خلاف الفرد وكل واحد منهما يسمى زوجا (sihah)؛ كل شيء اقترن أحدهما بالآخر فهما زوجان (tahdhib)؛ لكل قرينين زوج ولكل ما يقترن بآخر مماثلا له أو مضادا زوج (mufradat)

- **B003** eş kılmak, eş edinmek veya eşleştirmek — birine bir kadın eş sağlamak · bir kadınla evlenmek · bir kadınla evlenmek; belirli bir ağızda kullanılan kuruluş · eşleşme ve çift oluşturma · eşleştirme ve birbirine eş kılma · eşleşme ve çift oluşturma · kuşların birbiriyle eşleşmesi · eşleşmiş; bir eşi bulunan · onları eşleriyle bir araya getirmek · onları erkekler ve dişiler olarak eşleştirmek veya sınıflandırmak · kadının erkeği kendine eş edinmesi
  زوجته امرأة وتزوجت امرأة (sihah;tahdhib)؛ زوجناهم بحور عين أي قرناهم بهن (sihah;mufradat)؛ معنى يزوجهم يقرنهم وكل شيء اقترن أحدهما بالآخر فهما زوجان (tahdhib)
- **B004** tür, sınıf veya renk çeşidi — türler ve sınıflar · renk, tür veya sınıf · bir kumaş rengi · güzel bir bitki türü · çeşitli ve benzer bitki türleri · onları erkekler ve dişiler olarak eşleştirmek veya sınıflandırmak
  من كل زوج بهيج أراد به اللون (maqayis)؛ زوج من الثياب أي لون ومنها من كل زوج بهيج أي لون (ayn)؛ من كل ضرب من النبات حسن والزوج اللون وأزواج أي أنواع والزوج الصنف (tahdhib)؛ أزواجا من نبات شتى أي أنواعا متشابهة وثمانية أزواج أي أصناف (mufradat)
- **B005** benzerler ve aynı tutumu izleyen yoldaşlar — benzerler, denkler ve aynı tutumu izleyen yoldaşlar
  أزواجهم أي قرناءهم (sihah)؛ أزواجهم معناه نظراءهم ضرباءهم وعندي من هذا أزواج أي أمثال (tahdhib)؛ أزواجهم أي أقرانهم المقتدين بهم في أفعالهم وأشباها وأقرانا (mufradat)
- **B006** kapalı yolcu oturağına serilen desenli örtü — deve üzerindeki kapalı yolcu oturağına serilen desenli örtü

  الذكر الصلاة والدعاء والثناء (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الذكر قراءة القرآن والتسبيح والدعاء والشكر والطاعة (tahdhib)؛ ولذكر الله أكبر واذكروا الله (mufradat)

- **B009** hatırlatma, hatırlamayı sağlayan araç ve sıkça anma — hatırlatma, öğüt alma veya sıkça anma · hatırlatıcı · hatırlatma · ona o şeyi hatırlattı

- **B005** yerinden ayrılmayan ağır kişi veya bulunduğu yerde kalan hayvan — yerinden ya da evinden ayrılmayan ağır kimse · övgüde gözü pek kişi, yergide evinden ayrılmayan kimse için söylenen söz · su başında kalıp oradan ayrılmayan develer

  الأليس: البعير يحمل كل ما حمل (sihah)؛ تلايس الرجل: إذا كان حمولا حسن الخلق؛ وتلايست عن كذا وكذا: أي غمضت عنه؛ وفلان أليس دهثم: أي حسن الخلق (tahdhib)
- **B007** görüşü ve yargısı zayıf kişi — görüşü zayıf kimse
  الأليس الضعيف الرأي (ayn)
- **B008** ailesine karşı koruyucu kıskançlık göstermeyen erkeğe yönelik alaycı yergi — ailesine karşı koruyucu kıskançlık göstermediği için alay edilen erkek · ailesine karşı koruyucu kıskançlık göstermeyen erkeği alaya alan yergi sözü

- **B004** yılan ve yılanla ilgili adlandırmalar — yılan · erkek yılan · yılan bakıcısı · yılanlı toprak

- **B006** öldürmeyip sağ bırakma — kadınları sağ bırakıyor ve öldürmüyorlar

- **B007** esenlik, uzun ömür ve kalıcılık dileği — Tanrı sana yaşam, kalıcılık ve esenlik versin · karşılama ve esenlik dileği · yoruma göre bütün esenlik, kalıcılık ya da egemenlik Tanrı'nındır

  قولهم حي على الصلاة معناه هلم وأقبل والعرب تقول حي على الثريد وهو اسم لفعل الأمر (sihah)
- **B010** ortak soylu topluluk veya boylar birliği — soy topluluğu ya da boy · aynı soydan gelen bir topluluk
  الحي حي من العرب وبنو حي بطن من العرب (jamhara); الحي واحد أحياء العرب (sihah); الحي الواحد من أحياء العرب يقع على بني أب كثروا أم قلوا وعلى شعب يجمع القبائل (tahdhib)
- **B011** dişi canlının üreme organı veya döl yatağı — dişi insan ya da hayvanın üreme organı veya döl yatağı
  حياء الناقة وهو فرجها يمكن أن يكون من هذا (maqayis); الحياء أيضا رحم الناقة والجمع أحيية (sihah); الحي فرج المرأة وحياء الشاة والناقة والمرأة ممدود (tahdhib)
- **B012** yüz — yüz
  المحيا الوجه (sihah)
- **B013** yarar, iyilik ve yok olmaktan koruma — yarar, iyilik ve yok olmaktan koruma · çocuğu yaşatan besin
  في القصاص حياة أي منفعة وليس بفلان حياة أي ليس عنده نفع ولا خير (tahdhib); ولكم في القصاص حياة أي يرتدع بالقصاص ومن أحياها أي من نجاها من الهلاك (mufradat)
- **B014** yaşamla ilişkilendirilen erkek kişi adları — yaşamla ilişkilendirilen erkek kişi adları
  حيي اسم رجل (jamhara); حيوة اسم رجل (sihah); حيوة اسم رجل بسكون الياء (tahdhib); اسمه يحيى نبه أنه سماه بذلك من حيث إنه لم تمته الذنوب (mufradat)

## م و ت (root_001454): 75:40 ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ

- **B001** yaşamın ve canlı gücünün sona ermesi — ölüm; yaşamın sona ermesi · öldü; yaşamdan ayrıldı · öldü; yaşamdan ayrıldı · yakında ölecek kimse · ölmüş veya ölecek kimse · kesin ve gerçek ölüm
  أصل صحيح يدل على ذهاب القوة من الشيء (maqayis)؛ الموت خلاف الحياة (maqayis;sihah)؛ الموت معروف مات يموت موتا (jamhara)؛ الموت خلق من خلق الله (tahdhib)؛ أنواع الموت بحسب أنواع الحياة (mufradat)
- **B002** öldürme veya pişirerek keskinliğini giderme — öldürdü; gücünü giderdi · pişirerek keskinliğini giderin · içki pişirilip keskinliği giderildi
  أميتوها طبخا (maqayis)؛ أميتت الخمر طبخت (maqayis)؛ أماته الله وموته شدد للمبالغة (sihah)
- **B003** cansız şey; işlenmemiş veya sahipsiz arazi — işlenmemiş arazi; canlı olmayan şey · cansız şey; sahipsiz ve kullanılmayan arazi
  الموتان الأرض لم تحي بعد بزرع ولا إصلاح وكذلك الموات (maqayis)؛ الموات ما لا روح فيه (sihah)؛ الموات الأرض التي لا مالك لها ولا ينتفع بها أحد (sihah)؛ الموتان أن يبيع المتاع وكل شيء غير ذي روح (tahdhib)
- **B004** insanlar veya hayvan varlığı içinde ölüm görülmesi — insanlarda veya hayvanlarda görülen ölüm · mal veya hayvan varlığı içindeki ölüm
  وقع في الناس موتان (maqayis)؛ الموتان بالضم موت يقع في الماشية (sihah)؛ وقع في المال موتان وموات وهو الموت (tahdhib)
- **B005** çocuğu ölmüş ebeveyn veya ana hayvan — yavrusu ölmüş ana hayvan veya çocuğu ölmüş kadın · oğlu veya oğulları öldü
  ناقة مميت ومميتة للتي يموت ولدها (maqayis)؛ أماتت الناقة إذا مات ولدها فهي مميت ومميتة (sihah)؛ وكذلك المرأة (sihah)؛ أمات فلان إذا مات له ابن أو بنون (sihah)
- **B006** zekâ ve anlayıştan yoksunluk — zekâsı ve anlayışı kıt kimse · ne kadar anlayışsız!
  رجل موتان الفؤاد وامرأة موتانة (maqayis)؛ رجل موتان الفؤاد وامرأة موتانة الفؤاد (sihah)؛ رجل موتان الفؤاد إذا كان غير ذكي ولا فهم (tahdhib)
- **B007** usulüne uygun kesilmeden ölen yenilebilir hayvan — usulüne uygun kesilmeden ölmüş yenilebilir hayvan
  الميتة ما مات مما يؤكل لحمه إذا ذكي (maqayis)؛ الميتة ما لم تلحقه الذكاة (sihah)
- **B008** bir kez ölme veya ölüm biçimi — ölüm biçimi veya hali · bir kez ölme
  الموتة الواحدة من الموت (maqayis)؛ الميتة حال من الموت حسنة أو قبيحة (maqayis)؛ مات فلان ميتة حسنة (sihah)؛ الميتة الحال من أحوال الموت (tahdhib)
- **B009** ardından ayılınan geçici delilik, nöbet veya baygınlık — ardından ayılınan delilik benzeri hal, nöbet veya baygınlık
  الموتة شبه الجنون يعترى الإنسان (maqayis)؛ الموتة جنس من الجنون والصرع يعتري الإنسان (sihah)؛ الموتة الجنون (tahdhib)؛ الموتة الذي يصرع من الجنون أو غيره ثم يفيق (tahdhib)؛ الموتة شبه الغشية (tahdhib)
- **B010** bir işe kendini bütünüyle verme; savaşta ölümü göze alma [kalıp] — işe kendini bütünüyle veren · savaşta ölümü göze alarak dövüşen · ölümü gönüllü karşıladı
  المستميت للأمر المسترسل له (maqayis;sihah)؛ المستميت المستقتل الذي لا يبالي في الحرب من الموت (sihah)؛ استمات الرجل إذا طاب نفسا بالموت (tahdhib)؛ المستميت الذي يقاتل على الموت (tahdhib)
- **B011** ölmüş, deli veya alçakgönüllüymüş gibi davranma — gösteriş için aşırı alçakgönüllü görünen · canlıyken ölmüş gibi davrandı · deli veya alçakgönüllüymüş gibi davranan
  المتماوت من صفة الناسك المرائي (sihah)؛ المستميت الذي يتجان وليس بمجنون (tahdhib)؛ يتخاشع ويتواضع لهذا حتى يطعمه (tahdhib)؛ ضربته فتماوت إذا أرى أنه ميت وهو حي (tahdhib)؛ المتماوتون المراءون (tahdhib)
- **B012** rüzgârın dinmesi, kumaşın eskimesi veya insanın uyuması [kalıp] — rüzgâr dindi · kumaş eskidi ve yıprandı · adam uyudu ve hareketsizleşti
  الموت السكون (tahdhib)؛ ماتت الريح إذا سكنت (tahdhib)؛ مات الثوب ونام إذا بلي (tahdhib)؛ مات الرجل وهمد وهوم إذا نام (tahdhib)
- **B013** gerçeğe boyun eğme [kalıp] — adam gerçeğe boyun eğdi
  مات الرجل إذا خضع للحق (tahdhib)
- **B014** vurulmuş avın ölüp ölmediğini inceleme [kalıp] — avınızın ölüp ölmediğine bakın
  استميتوا صيدكم أي انظروا مات أم لا (tahdhib)؛ إذا أصيب فشك في موته (tahdhib)



===== _commentary/v16/work/s075/surah.r2/channels.md =====
(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s075/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s075 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1: Public Determination of Liability
- Semantic invariant: A disputed or hidden condition becomes answerable by oath, exact apportionment, reckoning, and an established record.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:1-4 anchors oath (`أُقْسِمُ`), reckoning (`يَحْسَبُ`), and capacity (`قَادِرِينَ`), while 75:12-15 anchor settlement, report, and excuses.
- Surprising reach: The opening oath expands into a procedural scene in which shares, measures, admissions, and defective pleas make liability publicly decidable.

#### Subchannel A. Sworn shares
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Claimants divide a matter into shares and secure the division by distributed oaths.
- Active motifs: allotted share (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); oath apportioned among its bearers (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); oath offered as a test or reassurance (`quranic:root_000154:B005/m01`); oath and swearing (`quranic:root_000076:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); surface anchors unavailable for ب ل ي and ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: The oath is not merely emphatic speech: its lexical construction distributes responsibility among parties, tests the hearer, and fixes each party's share.

#### Subchannel B. Counting to an exact proportion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A quantity is counted, valued, planned, and brought to its fitting measure.
- Active motifs: counting and accounting (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); bounded amount (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); measured planning and preparation (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); valuation (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); equal weight (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); equivalence between two things (`quranic:root_000766:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب); 75:4 `قَادِرِينَ`, `نُّسَوِّىَ` (ق د ر، س و ي); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م).
- Synthesis: Reckoning becomes a calibration scene: the object is counted, assigned a limit and value, and adjusted until its parts answer to an exact standard.

#### Subchannel C. Trial, admission, and recorded finding
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Examination reveals a condition, elicits an admission, and turns it into a delivered report despite a deficient plea.
- Active motifs: trial that manifests a person's state (`quranic:root_000154:B002/m01`); practical inspection and accountability (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`); established admission (`quranic:root_001215:B007/m01`); consequential report (`quranic:root_001464:B002/m01`); excuse pressed to its limit (`quranic:root_000995:B004/m01`); deficient excuse presented as justification (`quranic:root_000995:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ي.
- Synthesis: The scene moves from examination to a fixed declaration. What the subject tries to frame as an excuse is itself assessed as evidence and entered into the finding.

### 2. P1: Reconstructing a Fitted Body
- Semantic invariant: Dispersed bodily material is recovered as a complete, proportioned structure whose smallest terminals and joints are deliberately fitted.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:3-4 anchor gathering bones, capacity, proportioning, and fingertips.
- Surprising reach: Resurrection is materialized as both anatomical closure and exact craftwork: collecting, joining, carving, and matching components.

#### Subchannel A. From dispersed bones to an intact whole
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Scattered material is collected until no bodily part is absent and the frame again supports itself.
- Active motifs: joining what was dispersed (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`); complete body without loss (`quranic:root_000259:B009/m01`); force gathered until parts catch up (`quranic:root_000259:B010/m01`); hard bone (`quranic:root_001029:B005/m01`); structural support that keeps a thing standing (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `عِظَامَهُۥ` (ج م ع، ع ظ م); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م).
- Synthesis: Gathering is not a loose heap. The branch senses require completeness and coordinated force, turning recovered bones into a load-bearing whole.

#### Subchannel B. Closing the hand down to its terminals
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fingers and their tips are restored, drawn into a grasp, and matched to an exact bodily proportion.
- Active motifs: fingers and terminal joints (`quranic:root_000155:B003/m01`); fist formed by closing the fingers (`quranic:root_000259:B005/m01`); straight and complete bodily form (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`); fitting a thing to its proper measure (`quranic:root_001205:B006/m01`); making two ends meet (`quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `نَّجْمَعَ` (ج م ع); 75:4 `بَنَانَهُۥ`, `نُّسَوِّىَ`, `قَادِرِينَ` (ب ن ن، س و ي، ق د ر); 75:15 `أَلْقَىٰ` (ل ق ي).
- Synthesis: The hand scene narrows from the whole body to terminal precision: separate digits close into a grasp because their ends, joints, and proportions have been made to meet.

#### Subchannel C. Joinery, seams, and carved members
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An artisan aligns edges, joins skins or panels, carves wood, and installs upright components.
- Active motifs: sewing one hide or panel to another (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); adze for carving wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); wooden saddle-frame member (`quranic:root_001029:B009/m01`); standing tool or structural component (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); lateral line or paired edge (`quranic:root_000995:B008/m01`); joined endpoints (`quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:13 `قَدَّمَ` (ق د م); 75:3 `عِظَامَهُۥ` (ع ظ م); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ`, `أَلْقَىٰ` (ع ذ ر، ل ق ي).
- Synthesis: The lexical scene supplies a craft analogue for bodily reconstruction. Uprights and carved members only become a sound structure when their lateral edges and endpoints are accurately joined.

### 3. P1: Deliberate Breach
- Semantic invariant: An inward preference is consolidated into resolve, then crosses a boundary through forceful rupture or coordinated pressure.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:5 anchors willing (`يُرِيدُ`) and breaking bounds (`يَفْجُرَ`).
- Surprising reach: Moral defiance is rendered mechanically as accumulated force that opens a breach, and socially as pressure exerted with allies.

#### Subchannel A. Desire hardened into resolve
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A wished-for course gathers scattered deliberation into a settled intention to act.
- Active motifs: will and purposive inclination (`quranic:root_000610:B001/m01`); decisively gathered counsel (`quranic:root_000259:B003/m01`); rising with resolve to an undertaking (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); mind divided among courses (`quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`); intention held within the self (`quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `يُرِيدُ` (ر و د); 75:3,9 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `جُمِعَ` (ج م ع); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س).
- Synthesis: The process begins as preference, passes through divided consideration, and ends when the parts of counsel are gathered into a single rising resolve.

#### Subchannel B. Bursting through the limit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Accumulated force splits an opening and turns physical rupture into transgression.
- Active motifs: wide splitting and outflow (`quranic:root_001132:B001/m01`); deviation from right by tearing a boundary (`quranic:root_001132:B004/m01`); profanation in the battles of transgression (`quranic:root_001132:B006/m01`); daring advance (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); aimed intention toward a target (`quranic:root_000053:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `يَفْجُرَ`, `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ف ج ر، ء م م); 75:13 `قَدَّمَ` (ق د م).
- Synthesis: The moral operation retains the geometry of its physical root: pressure opens a channel through a limit, and deliberate advance converts that opening into violated order.

#### Subchannel C. Coaxing, coalition, and resistance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One party presses another toward an act, gathers allies, negotiates the undertaking, and meets organized resistance.
- Active motifs: contest of wills through coaxing (`quranic:root_000610:B002/m01`); coalition around an affair (`quranic:root_000259:B013/m01`); negotiation over the matter (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); confrontation and resistance (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); contentious prevention and dominance (`quranic:root_000152:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `يُرِيدُ` (ر و د); 75:3,9 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `جُمِعَ` (ج م ع); 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: The breach also has a social form. Resolve is induced, allies are assembled, and the projected act becomes a contested collective undertaking.

### 4. P1: Sight Driven from Flash to Occlusion
- Semantic invariant: Intense visibility first arrests the eye, then turns into impaired, recessed, or extinguished sight.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:7-9 anchor the transfixed eye, the eclipsed moon, and the conjunction of sun and moon; 75:14 anchors inward sight.
- Surprising reach: More light does not yield more vision: glare, mixed brightness, and preserved eye-form can culminate in blindness.

#### Subchannel A. A flash fixes the gaze
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A brilliant event startles the eye into a rigid, unblinking stare and overwhelms discrimination.
- Active motifs: lightning or polished brilliance (`quranic:root_000108:B001/m01`); startled, transfixed eye (`quranic:root_000108:B002/m01`); direct ocular seeing (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`); snow-bright glare that confounds sight (`quranic:root_001255:B005/m01`); human image reflected in the pupil (`quranic:root_000059:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:7 `بَرِقَ ٱلْبَصَرُ` (ب ر ق، ب ص ر); 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر); 75:3,5,10,13-14 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` (ء ن س).
- Synthesis: The eye becomes both observer and illuminated surface. The flash fixes it open, while glare erases the distinctions that sight was meant to recover.

#### Subchannel B. An intact eye loses its depth of sight
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The eye recedes or is extinguished while its external globe remains standing.
- Active motifs: sunken or blinded eye (`quranic:root_000410:B003/m01`); intact eyeball with sight gone (`quranic:root_001273:B021/m01`); roaming defect within the eye (`quranic:root_000610:B007/m01`); injurious gaze cast upon another (`quranic:root_001533:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:8 `خَسَفَ` (خ س ف); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:5 `يُرِيدُ` (ر و د); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س).
- Synthesis: Occlusion is not merely darkness outside the observer. The visual organ itself becomes a standing shell whose depth and efficacy have withdrawn.

#### Subchannel C. Light-bearing bodies become mixed surfaces
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Sunlight, moonlight, pale stone, and black-white surfaces lose discrete optical identities.
- Active motifs: sun and its radiance (`quranic:root_000818:B001/m01`); moon and moonlight (`quranic:root_001255:B001/m01`); black and white mixed in one surface (`quranic:root_000108:B005/m01`); pale, glinting stone (`quranic:root_000121:B007/m01`); lunar whiteness (`quranic:root_001255:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:7 `بَرِقَ`, `ٱلْبَصَرُ` (ب ر ق، ب ص ر); 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر); 75:9 `ٱلشَّمْسُ` (ش م س).
- Synthesis: The conjunction of luminaries is reframed as an optical merger: distinct sources and colors collapse into a single unstable surface that no longer guides sight.

### 5. P1: Stored Sky-Water Breaking Open
- Semantic invariant: Water or light held in a clouded enclosure accumulates, flashes, opens a channel, and spreads across or into the ground.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:5,7-9 anchor rupture, flash, sinking, moon, and sun.
- Surprising reach: The cosmic disturbance acquires the mechanics of a storm and flood, with concealment, release, spreading, and subsidence.

#### Subchannel A. A loaded cloud releases abundance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Layered cloud stores plentiful water until lightning marks its release.
- Active motifs: layered rain-cloud (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); cloud carrying hidden abundant water (`quranic:root_000410:B007/m01`); lightning flash (`quranic:root_000108:B001/m01`); gathered plentiful water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); abundance of water and pasture (`quranic:root_001255:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:8 `خَسَفَ` (خ س ف); 75:7 `بَرِقَ` (ب ر ق); 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر).
- Synthesis: Cloud layers function as a reservoir. The flash announces that stored abundance is no longer contained, converting celestial disruption into a release mechanism.

#### Subchannel B. Dawn and flood split a passage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Darkness or an enclosing surface splits, water surges through the opening, and soft ground swallows what enters it.
- Active motifs: broad fissure with erupting water (`quranic:root_001132:B001/m01`); dawn opening out of night (`quranic:root_001132:B002/m01`); sudden massed onrush (`quranic:root_001132:B003/m01`); dawn, wave, or water spreading like breath (`quranic:root_001533:B009/m01`); ground subsidence and engulfment (`quranic:root_000410:B001/m01`); wetness and remaining moisture (`quranic:root_000152:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `يَفْجُرَ` (ف ج ر); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:8 `خَسَفَ` (خ س ف); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Opening and engulfment form one hydraulic sequence: a seam gives way, the released mass spreads, and the receiving ground loses its stability.

### 6. P1: Flight Ending at a Fixed Station
- Semantic invariant: Panicked motion searches for route, defense, and helper, but every option contracts into an unavoidable place of settlement.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:10-12 move from the cry for escape, through denial of refuge, to settlement with the Lord.
- Surprising reach: Refuge is decomposed into mountain, weapons, minister, route, and encampment, only for each protective function to fail.

#### Subchannel A. Agitated escape
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A frightened person cries out, darts away, and tears at restraints without recovering direction.
- Active motifs: flight and place of escape (`quranic:root_001142:B001/m01`); foolish, hurried agitation (`quranic:root_001142:B005/m01`); violent shaking and tearing (`quranic:root_001142:B006/m01`); crowd in confused motion (`quranic:root_001142:B016/m01`); spoken cry (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:10 `ٱلْمَفَرُّ`, `يَقُولُ` (ف ر ر، ق و ل).
- Synthesis: Escape is not orderly travel. Its branch senses show acceleration without command: speech, tearing motion, and collective confusion all amplify the lack of a viable route.

#### Subchannel B. Stronghold, arms, and burden-bearer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The fugitive seeks a defensible height, equipment, and a deputy able to carry the load.
- Active motifs: mountain refuge (`quranic:root_001643:B001/m01`); weapons and war equipment (`quranic:root_001643:B003/m01`); minister who carries another's burden (`quranic:root_001643:B004/m01`); shield or armor (`quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`); leading ridge or mountain prow (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:13 `قَدَّمَ` (ق د م).
- Synthesis: Refuge is a composite defense system: terrain protects, arms resist, and a deputy shares weight. The denial of `وَزَرَ` removes all three functions at once.

#### Subchannel C. Route collapsing into settlement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Movement along an apparent road ends in a fixed basin or station where further travel is impossible.
- Active motifs: fixed settlement (`quranic:root_001215:B003/m01`); low basin where material settles (`quranic:root_001215:B006/m01`); enduring residence (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); station or place of standing (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); immobilization after movement (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); clear road to a destination (`quranic:root_001464:B007/m01`); arrival at the terminal limit (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:12 `رَبِّكَ`, `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ر ب ب، ق ر ر); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: The path remains intelligible, but it is not an escape path. It descends into the appointed station, where mobility gives way to fixed presence.

### 7. P1: The Interior Becoming Its Own Evidence
- Semantic invariant: The self's hidden thought is converted into sight, speech, report, and admission, while attempted coverings become further evidence.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:13-15 anchor notification, self-sight, and cast excuses.
- Surprising reach: Consciousness functions as witness, document, and inspected object at once.

#### Subchannel A. Self, mind, and inward sight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The living self contains intention and reason, yet also sees and verifies its own condition.
- Active motifs: living soul (`quranic:root_001533:B011/m01`); the thing's very self and essence (`quranic:root_001533:B012/m01`); reason and intention within the self (`quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`); one's own intimate self (`quranic:root_000059:B006/m01`); heart-sight, knowledge, and self-testimony (`quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:3,5,10,13-14 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` (ء ن س); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر).
- Synthesis: The witness is not external to the inspected object. Essence, intention, cognition, and testimony coincide within one self-observing participant.

#### Subchannel B. Hidden statement turned into report
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What is held inwardly is elicited, delimited, admitted, and delivered as consequential news.
- Active motifs: seeking out concealed news (`quranic:root_000318:B010/m01`); consequential report (`quranic:root_001464:B002/m01`); unspoken statement in the mind (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); a thing's own indication (`quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`); definition that fixes what a thing is (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); admission made stable (`quranic:root_001215:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء); 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر).
- Synthesis: Interior content is progressively externalized: inquiry retrieves it, its signs delimit it, and admission fixes it as a report that can no longer be withdrawn.

#### Subchannel C. Excuses cast as screens
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker throws out pleas and coverings, but their defects, traces, and discarded form expose rather than hide him.
- Active motifs: excuse seeking erasure of fault (`quranic:root_000995:B001/m01`); feigned or deficient plea (`quranic:root_000995:B005/m01`); accumulated defects and sins (`quranic:root_000995:B018/m01`); curtains called excuses (`quranic:root_000995:B019/m01`); casting something away or at another (`quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`); discarded, ownerless object (`quranic:root_001372:B006/m01`); excuse disclosed until blame is removed (`quranic:root_000154:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ`, `أَلْقَىٰ` (ع ذ ر، ل ق ي); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ي.
- Synthesis: The defense has material form: curtains are flung up and verbal objects cast outward. Once inspected as discarded artifacts, however, they preserve the marks of the very fault they were meant to screen.

### 8. P1: Front, Rear, and Deferred Position
- Semantic invariant: Moral sequence is expressed through spatial frontage, bodily advance, rear position, and delayed time.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:5 places action ahead, and 75:13 opposes what was sent forward to what was held back.
- Surprising reach: Temporal responsibility is made navigable as a body or vehicle with a front, rear, route, and time of arrival.

#### Subchannel A. Aiming and moving to the front
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A participant selects a target, faces it, and advances into the leading position.
- Active motifs: front and immediate proximity (`quranic:root_000053:B011/m01`); purposive aiming (`quranic:root_000053:B012/m01`); forward movement and precedence (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`); front member or vanguard (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m01`); intentional approach (`quranic:root_001207:B010/m01`); turning toward a direction (`quranic:root_000766:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:13 `قَدَّمَ` (ق د م); 75:4 `نُّسَوِّىَ` (س و ي).
- Synthesis: Intention acquires orientation. A target is selected, the body turns, and the deed moves from mere possibility into the front rank of realized action.

#### Subchannel B. Rear position and postponement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What is not advanced is placed behind, retained for later, or delayed in movement.
- Active motifs: later or other position (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); postponement to a later time (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); rear component opposed to the front (`quranic:root_000019:B003/m01`); delay and detention (`quranic:root_000076:B013/m01`); waiting before action (`quranic:root_001386:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:13 `أَخَّرَ` (ء خ ر); 75:2 `ٱللَّوَّامَةِ` (ل و م); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: Delay is not empty absence. The unadvanced deed occupies the rear of the sequence, where retention and postponement remain positive moral positions.

#### Subchannel C. A bounded day becomes an event
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Ordinary duration tightens into a marked interval and then into a severe occurrence.
- Active motifs: bounded daylight (`quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`); duration of any length (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); event-day and its severity (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); delayed age or interval (`quranic:root_000053:B008/m01`); standing noon (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); scorching midday (`quranic:root_001226:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1,6 `يَوْمِ`, `يَوْمُ` (ي و م); 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م).
- Synthesis: The day is first a measurable span, then a standing point, and finally an event whose intensity fills that span.

### 9. P1: Authority That Rules, Tends, and Teaches
- Semantic invariant: Legitimate authority combines ownership and power with sustained care, completion, and intelligible guidance.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:12 anchors `رَبِّكَ`, while 75:4 anchors capacity.
- Surprising reach: Lordship unfolds as an institutional ecology of ruler, steward, fosterer, scholar, guide, and burden-bearing deputy.

#### Subchannel A. Sovereign capacity and stewardship
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An owner rules a domain through effective power, guardianship, and delegated assistance.
- Active motifs: ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); effective capability and dominion (`quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`); guardianship and governance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); lesser ruler whose word carries force (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); minister carrying the ruler's burden (`quranic:root_001643:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:4 `قَادِرِينَ` (ق د ر); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر).
- Synthesis: Rule is presented as an operative relation, not a title: the ruler owns, can act, maintains the charge, speaks decisively, and assigns burdens without surrendering sovereignty.

#### Subchannel B. Fostered completion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A dependent is fed, raised, repaired, and brought stage by stage to completion.
- Active motifs: repair, nurture, and gradual completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); foster-child and custodian (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); mother as bearer and educator (`quranic:root_000053:B001/m01`); origin and gathering center (`quranic:root_000053:B002/m01`); sustaining support (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م).
- Synthesis: The ruler's relation to the subject is also developmental. Authority repairs and completes what depends on it, as a mother or guardian sustains an immature charge.

#### Subchannel C. Scholar, messenger, and guide-road
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Knowledge is cultivated, delivered through an authorized messenger, and embodied in a traversable route.
- Active motifs: cultivated learned authority (`quranic:root_000532:B003/m01`); messenger who reports from God (`quranic:root_001464:B003/m01`); leader or model followed by a community (`quranic:root_000053:B009/m01`); sincere care for a matter (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`); clear road that conducts the traveler (`quranic:root_001464:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء); 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل).
- Synthesis: Guidance joins epistemic and spatial roles. The cultivated teacher authorizes a report, the model gives it social direction, and the report becomes a road rather than inert information.

### 10. P1: Gestation as Continuity of Embodied Life
- Semantic invariant: Life persists by gathering generative material, fixing it in a protected interior, bringing it to birth, and tending it toward mature form.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:2-4 anchor living self, bodily gathering, proportion, and extremities.
- Surprising reach: The promised reconstitution is pressured by an earlier bodily construction cycle: conception, implantation, birth, nurture, and growth.

#### Subchannel A. Conception, attachment, and birth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Sexual union begins a pregnancy, generative material settles and holds, and child and postpartum blood emerge.
- Active motifs: sexual joining (`quranic:root_000259:B006/m01`); pregnancy held within the woman (`quranic:root_000259:B007/m01`); rapid conception (`quranic:root_001372:B003/m01`); semen fixed and pregnancy established (`quranic:root_001215:B013/m01`); childbirth and postpartum blood (`quranic:root_001533:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3 `نَّجْمَعَ` (ج م ع); 75:15 `أَلْقَىٰ` (ل ق ي); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س).
- Synthesis: Collection becomes biological containment: union gathers material, settlement gives it duration, and birth converts the hidden continuity into a visible body.

#### Subchannel B. Mother, fosterer, and newborn
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A newborn belongs to a maternal and foster household that feeds, protects, and completes it.
- Active motifs: mother and nurturer (`quranic:root_000053:B001/m01`); newborn girl or bondmaid (`quranic:root_000053:B014/m01`); foster-child and caregiver (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); recently delivered animal kept near milk and shelter (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); newborn delivered through childbirth (`quranic:root_001533:B005/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س).
- Synthesis: Birth does not complete the life-cycle by itself. Maternal origin and fostered care maintain the vulnerable body until it can sustain its own form.

#### Subchannel C. Stature and mature completion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The growing body reaches strength, stature, and a balanced set of extremities.
- Active motifs: completion of youth and strength (`quranic:root_000766:B005/m01`); bodily stature and form (`quranic:root_000053:B006/m01`); upright bodily height (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); terminal members and finger joints (`quranic:root_000155:B003/m01`); young woman in fresh maturity (`quranic:root_000610:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:4 `نُّسَوِّىَ`, `بَنَانَهُۥ` (س و ي، ب ن ن); 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ`, `يُرِيدُ` (ء م م، ر و د); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م).
- Synthesis: Growth culminates not in bulk but in proportion: height, strength, and the body's smallest terminals arrive together as a mature configuration.

### 11. P1: Coverings That Become Seams and Restraints
- Semantic invariant: Material first folds or screens, then joins along edges, and finally constrains the body it covers.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:15 anchors cast excuses, while 75:3-4 and 75:14 anchor body, fingertips, and sight.
- Surprising reach: Verbal coverings acquire textile mechanics, and protective or concealing material can harden into a binding apparatus.

#### Subchannel A. Folded and discarded screens
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Damp cloth or hide is folded, used as a screen, and then cast off as a low-value remnant.
- Active motifs: folding material while moisture remains (`quranic:root_000152:B007/m01`); first fold of a garment (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`); curtains named as excuses (`quranic:root_000995:B019/m01`); discarded cloth or object (`quranic:root_001372:B006/m01`); moisture retained in material (`quranic:root_000152:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ`, `أَلْقَىٰ` (ع ذ ر، ل ق ي); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: The excuse-screen is treated as handled material: folded while still marked by what it covered, then discarded without losing those traces.

#### Subchannel B. Stitched margins
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Two panels are aligned along thick edges and sewn into one covering.
- Active motifs: thick margin between joined panels (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m02`); paired lateral line or border (`quranic:root_000995:B008/m01`); first garment fold (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`); bringing endpoints together (`quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ`, `أَلْقَىٰ` (ع ذ ر، ل ق ي); 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م).
- Synthesis: A covering becomes coherent only at its boundaries. Margins, lines, and endpoints determine whether separate pieces actually conceal as one surface.

#### Subchannel C. Covering hardened into restraint
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A covering or defensive layer tightens into armor, hand-to-neck fetter, or war equipment.
- Active motifs: fetter joining hands to neck (`quranic:root_000259:B008/m01`); hand-to-neck shackles (`quranic:root_000995:B020/m01`); armor or shield (`quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`); equipment carried for war (`quranic:root_001643:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3,9 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `جُمِعَ` (ج م ع); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر).
- Synthesis: Material meant to cover or defend can reverse role and immobilize. The same logic of joining becomes the logic of binding.

### 12. P1: Sworn Truce Under Negotiation
- Semantic invariant: Opposed parties suspend conflict through oath and covenant, then maintain the settlement through negotiation, alliance, and delegated support.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:1-2 anchor oath, while 75:3,9-12 anchor assembly, speech, burden-bearing, and lordship.
- Surprising reach: The oath opens a political scene in which peace is a maintained relation rather than an isolated utterance.

#### Subchannel A. Oath, covenant, and ceasefire
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Parties bind themselves by oath to a covenant that creates protected relations and suspends hostilities.
- Active motifs: ceasefire called `قسامة` (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`); apportioned oath (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); covenant and protected compact (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); oath and pledge (`quranic:root_000076:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: Swearing creates a social interval in which violence is held back. The compact endures because obligation is divided and protection is mutually recognized.

#### Subchannel B. Parley, coalition, and organized opposition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Representatives confer, assemble supporters, and negotiate against a resisting side.
- Active motifs: negotiation over an affair (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); coalition around a shared matter (`quranic:root_000259:B013/m01`); resistance and contest (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); ministerial support (`quranic:root_001643:B004/m01`); open hostility (`quranic:root_000818:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل); 75:3,9 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `جُمِعَ` (ج م ع); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر); 75:9 `ٱلشَّمْسُ` (ش م س).
- Synthesis: The truce is surrounded by active politics. Speech tests terms, coalitions supply leverage, and delegated supporters keep opposition from collapsing into immediate violence.

### 13. P2: Speech Assembled Before It Is Released
- Semantic invariant: Controlled speech is physically articulated, gathered into an integral text, followed in sequence, and then disclosed through an authorized explanation.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:16-19 anchor tongue movement, gathering, recitation, following, and clarification.
- Surprising reach: Revelation behaves simultaneously like bodily articulation, component assembly, patterned craft, and message delivery.

#### Subchannel A. Tongue movement becomes articulate speech
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The speech organ moves, finds the positions of sounds, and turns breath and language into intelligible utterance.
- Active motifs: movement against stillness (`quranic:root_000312:B001/m01`); tongue as speech organ (`quranic:root_001355:B001/m01`); fluent articulation and argument (`quranic:root_001355:B003/m01`); language carried by the tongue (`quranic:root_001355:B006/m01`); tongue landing continuously on letter positions (`quranic:root_000152:B006/m01`); tongue as instrument of saying (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16 `تُحَرِّكْ`, `لِسَانَكَ` (ح ر ك، ل س ن); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Utterance is a coordinated motor event. Motion alone is insufficient: the tongue must repeatedly strike ordered phonetic positions so that language emerges rather than noise.

#### Subchannel B. Separate units gathered into a complete reading
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Discrete verbal units are collected, made whole, and delivered as a readable composition.
- Active motifs: collecting dispersed parts (`quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`); completeness without loss (`quranic:root_000259:B009/m01`); gathering and joining (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); collected wording and its reading (`quranic:root_001211:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:17 `جَمْعَهُۥ`, `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ج م ع، ق ر ء); 75:18 `قَرَأْنَٰهُ`, `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء).
- Synthesis: Gathering is textual integrity, not accumulation. Components are joined until the wording is complete enough to be recited as one composition.

#### Subchannel C. Following a patterned sequence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The recipient tracks a leading recitation step by step and reproduces its ordered pattern.
- Active motifs: following and taking a leader as model (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`); catching up with what precedes (`quranic:root_000175:B002/m01`); tracing an occurrence or mark in sequence (`quranic:root_000175:B003/m01`); uninterrupted succession (`quranic:root_000175:B004/m01`); path or model for composition (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m01`); poetic pattern followed by another text (`quranic:root_001211:B005/m01`); letters joined within one word (`quranic:root_001365:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع), `قَرَأْنَٰهُ`, `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف).
- Synthesis: Following is a reproducible textual operation: the recipient stays behind the leading act, tracks each unit, and preserves both succession and formal pattern.

#### Subchannel D. Meaning disclosed and delivered
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A complete utterance is opened to understanding and conveyed through a capable messenger.
- Active motifs: meaning revealed by speech or sign (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`); thing becoming manifest and distinct (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`); delivering a message (`quranic:root_000151:B002/m01`); eloquence that reaches its intended meaning (`quranic:root_000151:B004/m01`); message conveyed by another's tongue (`quranic:root_001355:B008/m01`); formal salutation transmitted in its wording (`quranic:root_001211:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن); 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ` (ب ل غ); 75:16 `لِسَانَكَ` (ل س ن); 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء).
- Synthesis: Explanation completes transmission by crossing a second gap: assembled sound reaches the recipient, then disclosed meaning reaches understanding.

### 14. P2: Due Time Against Premature Taking
- Semantic invariant: A process has an appointed maturity and endpoint; haste takes or releases it before that condition has formed.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:16,20 oppose haste and the immediate, 75:21 names the later, and 75:26 marks arrival at a bodily limit.
- Surprising reach: Temporal preference is embodied as unripe fruit, premature birth, hurried provisions, and a shortened route.

#### Subchannel A. Acting before ripeness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A participant accelerates a process, handles it before maturity, or forces delivery before its proper term.
- Active motifs: rushing and taking before due time (`quranic:root_000987:B001/m01`); unripe freshness and action before readiness (`quranic:root_000114:B001/m01`); premature delivery whose offspring survives (`quranic:root_000987:B007/m01`); shortened fast route (`quranic:root_000987:B008/m01`); extending the reins to increase speed (`quranic:root_000151:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:24 `بَاسِرَةٌ` (ب س ر); 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ` (ب ل غ).
- Synthesis: Haste changes the object's state, not just the clock. Fruit is handled unripe, gestation is cut short, and the traveler sacrifices process to speed.

#### Subchannel B. Waiting for the appointed arrival
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A later condition is deferred, watched for, and allowed to reach its limit.
- Active motifs: postponement to a later time (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); waiting and granting respite (`quranic:root_001520:B002/m01`); appointed time that approaches or is delayed (`quranic:root_001210:B005/m01`); delay and detention (`quranic:root_000076:B013/m01`); reaching a terminal point (`quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`); abiding until completion (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:21 `ٱلْءَاخِرَةَ` (ء خ ر); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌۭ` (ن ظ ر); 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ` (ب ل غ); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: The later condition is not inert postponement. It is an approaching limit whose integrity depends on being awaited rather than seized early.

#### Subchannel C. Hurried ration and immediate fullness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler takes a small container and quickly prepared ration, drinks to fullness, and consumes what is immediately available.
- Active motifs: small light waterskin (`quranic:root_000987:B004/m01`); provision prepared in haste (`quranic:root_000987:B005/m01`); drinking until full (`quranic:root_000286:B006/m01`); large storage jar (`quranic:root_000286:B007/m01`); water bubbles and surface movement (`quranic:root_000286:B008/m01`); wetness and water in a skin (`quranic:root_000152:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:20 `تُحِبُّونَ` (ح ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Love of the immediate is materialized as travel consumption: portable water and ready food satisfy at once, but their scale is bounded by the short journey they serve.

### 15. P2: The Face as Exposed Outcome
- Semantic invariant: The face presents inward orientation and anticipated outcome as visible radiance, contraction, damage, or status.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:22-25 contrast radiant faces looking to their Lord with grim faces expecting a crushing event.
- Surprising reach: Countenance is simultaneously a viewing organ, viewed surface, social rank, and diagnostic display.

#### Subchannel A. Radiance on a visible surface
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A face becomes bright, fresh, and visibly pleasing, like pure metal or flourishing growth.
- Active motifs: face as exposed front (`quranic:root_001630:B001/m01`); fresh beauty and radiance (`quranic:root_001516:B001/m01`); pure gold and refined ingot (`quranic:root_001516:B002/m01`); pleasing visible appearance (`quranic:root_001520:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:22 `وُجُوهٌ`, `نَّاضِرَةٌ` (و ج ه، ن ض ر); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ` (ن ظ ر).
- Synthesis: Radiance is a surface condition with material depth: freshness, polished purity, and pleasing appearance make the face visibly answer to its state.

#### Subchannel B. Contraction, pallor, and facial injury
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fear contracts the face, discolors it, and exposes the prospect of a blow.
- Active motifs: grimace and severe aversion (`quranic:root_000114:B002/m01`); pallor or defect left by a glance (`quranic:root_001520:B007/m01`); striking the face (`quranic:root_001630:B013/m01`); double-faced exterior opposed to interior (`quranic:root_001630:B015/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:24 `وُجُوهٌ`, `بَاسِرَةٌ` (و ج ه، ب س ر); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ` (ن ظ ر).
- Synthesis: The grim face is not a generic emotion label. It contracts under expectation, bears visible discoloration, and anticipates the impact that will make the inner fear bodily.

#### Subchannel C. Facing, gazing, and being faced
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The face turns toward a destination, meets what stands opposite it, and directs the pupil in sustained regard.
- Active motifs: direction and destination (`quranic:root_001630:B002/m01`); face-to-face encounter (`quranic:root_001630:B003/m01`); eye or insight directed to an object (`quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`); pupil and place of sight (`quranic:root_001520:B008/m01`); sovereignty of the Lord (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); movement to a terminal goal (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:22,24 `وُجُوهٌ` (و ج ه); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ`, `رَبِّهَا` (ن ظ ر، ر ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: Looking is an oriented relation involving surface, organ, direction, and object. The face does not merely shine; it is aligned toward the authority before whom its state appears.

### 16. P2: Expectation as Inspection Under Uncertainty
- Semantic invariant: A future outcome is inferred from signs, watched for at its likely place, and tested through reciprocal examination.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:23 anchors looking, while 75:25 and 75:28 anchor expectation or conviction.
- Surprising reach: Inner expectation expands into the practices of lookout, custodian, examiner, and disputant.

#### Subchannel A. Sign-based certainty and unstable supposition
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person reads indications that range from strong certainty to weak conjecture about an untrusted outcome.
- Active motifs: certainty grounded in a sign (`quranic:root_000969:B001/m01`); weak supposition (`quranic:root_000969:B003/m01`); matter whose result cannot be trusted (`quranic:root_000969:B006/m01`); statement functioning as supposition (`quranic:root_001272:B011/m01`); event becoming manifest (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:25,28 `تَظُنُّ`, `ظَنَّ` (ظ ن ن); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل); 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن).
- Synthesis: Expectation is graded rather than binary. Signs can establish conviction, remain conjectural, or point toward an outcome whose reliability itself is in question.

#### Subchannel B. Lookout, likely place, and reciprocal examination
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A custodian watches a likely location and confers with another examiner about what the signs mean.
- Active motifs: likely location of the sought thing (`quranic:root_000969:B002/m01`); watcher and custodian (`quranic:root_001520:B009/m01`); mutual examination and debate (`quranic:root_001520:B010/m01`); facing arrangement with mutual visibility (`quranic:root_001520:B003/m01`); visible proof (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:25,28 `تَظُنُّ`, `ظَنَّ` (ظ ن ن); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ` (ن ظ ر); 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن).
- Synthesis: The mental act becomes a field procedure: identify the likely site, maintain observation, and test the reading through another observer's opposing view.

#### Subchannel C. A fatal glance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Time or fate appears to look at a person, leaving pallor and destruction in the wake of its gaze.
- Active motifs: fatal gaze of time (`quranic:root_001520:B005/m01`); discoloring or injurious glance (`quranic:root_001520:B007/m01`); grim facial contraction (`quranic:root_000114:B002/m01`); crushing calamity (`quranic:root_001169:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ` (ن ظ ر); 75:24 `بَاسِرَةٌ` (ب س ر); 75:25 `فَاقِرَةٌۭ` (ف ق ر).
- Synthesis: The direction of looking reverses. The threatened face is not only an observer; it becomes the object of a gaze whose visible trace is pallor and whose outcome is ruin.

### 17. P2: Terminal Anatomy Under Failed Treatment
- Semantic invariant: Illness reaches the throat and load-bearing frame, speech and breath constrict, treatment is summoned, and the body nevertheless undergoes a breaking event.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:25-29 anchor the spine-breaking calamity, clavicles, call for a healer, separation, and entwined legs.
- Surprising reach: The death scene is anatomically distributed across throat, shoulder, hip, spine, vein, and tongue, while healing senses remain active precisely where cure fails.

#### Subchannel A. Illness reaches the clavicular gate
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worsening condition advances until breath and bodily force reach the collarbones and shoulder girdle.
- Active motifs: illness becoming severe (`quranic:root_000151:B008/m01`); clavicle linking throat and shoulder (`quranic:root_002011:B001/m01`); upper chest where breath climbs (`quranic:root_000588:B005/m01`); withers and neck junction (`quranic:root_000312:B002/m01`); hip and pelvic extremities (`quranic:root_000312:B003/m01`); articulated vertebral column (`quranic:root_001169:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ`, `ٱلتَّرَاقِىَ` (ب ل غ، ت ر ق); 75:27 `رَاقٍ` (ر ق ي); 75:16 `تُحَرِّكْ` (ح ر ك); 75:25 `فَاقِرَةٌۭ` (ف ق ر).
- Synthesis: The terminal threshold is a structural gate. Breath climbs through the upper chest while failure propagates along the body's articulated load-bearing axis.

#### Subchannel B. Healer and recovery are called but do not arrive
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Observers name the crisis, seek an incanting healer, and reach for recovery senses that the scene immediately reverses.
- Active motifs: spoken call (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); protective incantation and healer (`quranic:root_000588:B003/m01`); convalescence after illness (`quranic:root_000152:B003/m01`); recovery when disease departs (`quranic:root_001148:B010/m01`); action effected upon a patient (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:27 `قِيلَ`, `رَاقٍ` (ق و ل، ر ق ي); 75:25 `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ع ل); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Cure remains semantically present as an unrealized branch of the scene. Calling the healer activates recovery, but the next operation is separation rather than the disease's departure.

#### Subchannel C. A blow that breaks the frame
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Coercive force strikes the neck or shoulder, cuts tissue, and acts like a calamity that breaks the spine.
- Active motifs: spine-breaking calamity (`quranic:root_001169:B008/m01`); blow or cut at neck and withers (`quranic:root_000312:B004/m01`); cutting flesh or opening a wound (`quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`); imposed or coercive action (`quranic:root_000114:B004/m01`); act causing its object to undergo change (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:25 `فَاقِرَةٌۭ`, `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ق ر، ف ع ل); 75:16 `تُحَرِّكْ` (ح ر ك); 75:21 `تَذَرُونَ` (و ذ ر); 75:24 `بَاسِرَةٌ` (ب س ر).
- Synthesis: `فَاقِرَة` is rendered as an applied force with an anatomical path. It crosses tissue and shoulder until the body's articulated support fails.

#### Subchannel D. Breath and speech constrict together
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Ascending breath reaches the throat while tongue and limb grow heavy and articulation fails.
- Active motifs: breath climbing at the collarbone (`quranic:root_000588:B005/m01`); tongue or person made heavy and slow (`quranic:root_001365:B004/m01`); cut tongue-tip (`quranic:root_001355:B005/m01`); neck struck or severed (`quranic:root_000312:B004/m01`); bodily connection at the clavicle (`quranic:root_002011:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:27 `رَاقٍ` (ر ق ي); 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف); 75:16 `لِسَانَكَ`, `تُحَرِّكْ` (ل س ن، ح ر ك); 75:26 `ٱلتَّرَاقِىَ` (ت ر ق).
- Synthesis: Speech and life share the same narrowing passage. As breath rises, tongue and neck lose coordinated motion, making failed articulation part of the terminal anatomy.

### 18. P2: Separation Followed by Terminal Rebinding
- Semantic invariant: Connected parts and relations are distinguished and severed, after which the body is folded, wound, and gathered into a final covering.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:28-29 anchor separation and one leg winding around the other.
- Surprising reach: Death is not simple dispersal: cutting and wrapping are successive operations that undo one set of bonds and impose another.

#### Subchannel A. Distinguishing and severing joined parts
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A connection becomes visible at its boundary, then is cut into separate bodies, routes, or pieces.
- Active motifs: separation after connection (`quranic:root_000170:B001/m01`); bodily member opening away from its neighbor (`quranic:root_000170:B008/m01`); distinguishing and severing (`quranic:root_001148:B001/m01`); scattering into parts (`quranic:root_001148:B002/m01`); split or detached piece (`quranic:root_001148:B004/m01`); parting line in hair (`quranic:root_001148:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق).
- Synthesis: Separation first identifies the line of connection, then acts on it. Clarification and cutting share a boundary-making operation, but the terminal scene carries that operation into the body.
