#### Subchannel B. Fear makes the heart come apart
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The person recognizes impending departure, and fear internally disperses the heart before physical separation completes.
- Active motifs: heart scattered by severe fear (`quranic:root_001148:B009/m01`); certainty from an indication (`quranic:root_000969:B001/m01`); fearful or weak supposition (`quranic:root_000969:B003/m01`); unspoken statement held inwardly (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:28 `ظَنَّ`, `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ظ ن ن، ف ر ق); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل).
- Synthesis: Recognition has a bodily analogue: conviction of departure does not stabilize the person but breaks inward cohesion before the limbs are bound.

#### Subchannel C. Winding limbs and shrouding the body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Legs and flesh draw together, a twisted member disables motion, and cloth folds around the body.
- Active motifs: wrapping and shrouding (`quranic:root_001365:B001/m01`); flesh making thighs press together (`quranic:root_001365:B005/m01`); twisted vein disabling a limb (`quranic:root_001365:B007/m01`); leg as load-bearing member (`quranic:root_000762:B003/m01`); clavicular connector (`quranic:root_002011:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ ٱلسَّاقُ بِٱلسَّاقِ` (ل ف ف، س و ق); 75:26 `ٱلتَّرَاقِىَ` (ت ر ق).
- Synthesis: Winding moves from internal anatomy to outer cloth. The body loses independent limb motion, closes upon itself, and receives a new binding in place of life-coordination.

#### Subchannel D. A group splits, then mixes under one cover
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A gathered body of people breaks into separate parties, whose remnants are then mixed into another composite group.
- Active motifs: assembled crowd or mixture (`quranic:root_000259:B002/m01`); separated party or flock (`quranic:root_001148:B005/m01`); moving aside in dispersal (`quranic:root_001148:B016/m01`); mixed people folded into one group (`quranic:root_001365:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:17 `جَمْعَهُۥ` (ج م ع); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف).
- Synthesis: The same gather-separate-wrap sequence has a social scale. A collective loses its first organization, then its displaced members are absorbed into a different mixture.

### 19. P2: A Driven Procession Reaching Its Master
- Semantic invariant: Participants are compelled into ordered movement along a route whose forks disappear as it reaches the owning authority.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:29-30 anchor legs, driving, and movement to the Lord.
- Surprising reach: The final conveyance resembles herd movement, a rear-guarded procession, and route-following rather than self-directed travel.

#### Subchannel A. Herding into single-file succession
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A driver urges bodies forward until they follow one another in a common track with a defined rear.
- Active motifs: driving and herding (`quranic:root_000762:B001/m01`); succession in one trace (`quranic:root_000762:B012/m01`); rear of an advancing body (`quranic:root_000762:B013/m01`); catching what goes ahead (`quranic:root_000175:B002/m01`); continuous following (`quranic:root_000175:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29-30 `ٱلسَّاقُ`, `ٱلْمَسَاقُ` (س و ق); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع).
- Synthesis: The procession is ordered from outside. Each participant follows the same trace, and even the rear belongs to a movement whose direction has already been imposed.

#### Subchannel B. Route, fork, and terminal authority
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A marked route passes a fork, aligns travelers toward one direction, and reaches the sovereign destination.
- Active motifs: road and directed course (`quranic:root_001630:B002/m01`); physical path and intended course (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m02`); branching road (`quranic:root_001148:B006/m02`); attainment of the endpoint (`quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`); sovereign owner (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); terminal limit (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:22,24 `وُجُوهٌ` (و ج ه); 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ` (ب ل غ); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: Multiple route senses contract toward a single goal. The fork remains lexically visible, but terminal authority determines which direction is realized.

### 20. P2: Marketplace Claims and Withheld Rights
- Semantic invariant: Goods, labor, and obligations circulate through a market until a claimant follows the debt and confronts whoever withholds it.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:18 anchors following, 75:25 action performed upon another, and 75:29-30 driving or conveyance.
- Surprising reach: Compelled movement to judgment is pressured by a commercial settlement scene of transfer, liability, collection, and refusal.

#### Subchannel A. Market, workers, and public transfer
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods are driven to a market where workers and negotiating parties arrange exchange.
- Active motifs: marketplace and sale (`quranic:root_000762:B005/m01`); common subjects in contrast to ruler (`quranic:root_000762:B007/m01`); workers in clay, digging, or carpentry (`quranic:root_001167:B003/m01`); negotiation over an affair (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); action exchanged between two parties (`quranic:root_001167:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29-30 `ٱلسَّاقُ`, `ٱلْمَسَاقُ` (س و ق); 75:25 `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ع ل); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل).
- Synthesis: Conveyance becomes economic circulation. Driven goods reach a public place where labor, price, and reciprocal action are made negotiable.

#### Subchannel B. Claimant pursuing a liability
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A right adheres to its debtor; the claimant follows it, while the debtor blocks, wraps, or redirects the demand.
- Active motifs: claimant pursuing a right or debt (`quranic:root_000175:B005/m01`); liability that remains attached (`quranic:root_000175:B006/m01`); preventing another from his right (`quranic:root_000312:B006/m01`); wrapping and withholding a right (`quranic:root_001365:B006/m01`); turning a petitioner away (`quranic:root_001630:B014/m01`); imposing judgment on another (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع); 75:16 `تُحَرِّكْ` (ح ر ك); 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف); 75:22,24 `وُجُوهٌ` (و ج ه); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل).
- Synthesis: Obligation behaves like a follower that cannot be shaken off. Attempts to block or wrap the right only bring the dispute before an enforcing decision.

### 21. P2: Gestational Containment and Timed Release
- Semantic invariant: A reproductive interior gathers blood or offspring, holds it through a measured cycle, verifies its condition, and finally releases or separates what it contained.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:17-18 anchor gathering and `قرء`, while 75:26-28 anchor arrival and separation.
- Surprising reach: Textual gathering and recitation retain a source-attested uterine scene of collection, cyclic timing, testing, and release.

#### Subchannel A. Womb gathering and implantation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The womb collects generative material and holds a pregnancy whose expected offspring is not yet visible.
- Active motifs: pregnancy retained within a woman or animal (`quranic:root_000259:B007/m01`); womb gathering blood or embryo (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); gathered pregnancy in the womb (`quranic:root_001211:B003/m01`); menstrual and purified phases (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); woman from whom offspring is expected (`quranic:root_000969:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:17 `جَمْعَهُۥ`, `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ج م ع، ق ر ء); 75:18 `قَرَأْنَٰهُ`, `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); 75:25,28 `تَظُنُّ`, `ظَنَّ` (ظ ن ن).
- Synthesis: Gathering is a protected biological operation. Cyclic phases provide the time structure within which an unseen but expected body can be retained.

#### Subchannel B. Verification, premature birth, and postpartum separation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Pregnancy is tested, guarded through its term, or released prematurely; labor separates mother, offspring, and postpartum matter.
- Active motifs: testing whether an animal conceived (`quranic:root_001210:B009/m01`); holding a woman for reproductive verification (`quranic:root_001210:B010/m01`); premature delivery (`quranic:root_000987:B007/m01`); laboring animal separating from the herd (`quranic:root_001148:B008/m01`); postpartum food or remedy (`quranic:root_001148:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق).
- Synthesis: Release is governed by verification and term. Haste becomes premature birth, while separation becomes the labor event that reorganizes every participant's relation.

### 22. P2: Ripeness Written on the Face
- Semantic invariant: Plant maturation, moisture, and growth provide a material scale for facial freshness or premature contraction.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:20 anchors desire for the immediate, while 75:22-24 oppose fresh and grim faces.
- Surprising reach: Eschatological countenance is read through horticultural condition: seed, moisture, ripeness, density, and withering.

#### Subchannel A. Seed, freshness, and premature harvest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A seed develops toward fresh growth, but premature handling arrests ripening and produces a contracted result.
- Active motifs: seed that germinates (`quranic:root_000286:B001/m01`); fresh radiant condition of face or tree (`quranic:root_001516:B001/m01`); unripe fruit or plant (`quranic:root_000114:B001/m01`); plant tended toward growth (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); heart's dark kernel or fruit (`quranic:root_000286:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:20 `تُحِبُّونَ` (ح ب ب); 75:22 `نَّاضِرَةٌ` (ن ض ر); 75:24 `بَاسِرَةٌ` (ب س ر); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب).
- Synthesis: The contrast in faces is temporal as well as optical. One surface has reached living freshness; the other bears the contraction of a process seized before ripeness.

#### Subchannel B. Intertwined growth and scattered growth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Cultivated growth either interlaces into a dense stand or separates into sparse disconnected patches.
- Active motifs: intertwined trees and vegetation (`quranic:root_001365:B002/m01`); scattered, discontinuous plants (`quranic:root_001148:B014/m01`); plant tended toward persistence (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); fruit repositioned and bedded for growth (`quranic:root_001630:B012/m01`); plant named for haste (`quranic:root_000987:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:22,24 `وُجُوهٌ` (و ج ه); 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل).
- Synthesis: Interlacing and separation become botanical outcomes. Patient tending produces connected density, while haste and parting leave isolated growth.

#### Subchannel C. Irrigation and visible fullness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is stored, poured to fullness, and appears as surface bubbles or living moisture.
- Active motifs: drinking or watering to fullness (`quranic:root_000286:B006/m01`); storage jar (`quranic:root_000286:B007/m01`); bubbles and tracks on water (`quranic:root_000286:B008/m01`); plentiful gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); wetness and dew (`quranic:root_000152:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:20 `تُحِبُّونَ` (ح ب ب); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Freshness depends on an unseen supply system. Stored water becomes visible only when fullness reaches the surface, paralleling how inward condition appears on the face.

### 23. P2: Oversight from Ruler to Subject
- Semantic invariant: Authority directs a community through ownership, care, observation, delegated speech, and an ordered body of subjects.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:18 commands following, while 75:23 and 75:30 name the Lord as terminal authority.
- Surprising reach: The pericope's transmission and procession are also administrative: ruler, messenger, watcher, workers, and populace occupy distinct roles.

#### Subchannel A. Ruler, custodian, and authorized speaker
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A sovereign maintains a charge through cultivated care, a watchful custodian, and a subordinate whose word carries authority.
- Active motifs: sovereign ownership (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); gradual repair and care (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); watcher and entrusted custodian (`quranic:root_001520:B009/m01`); subordinate ruler with effective speech (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); successive rulers (`quranic:root_000175:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:23 `نَاظِرَةٌ` (ن ظ ر); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع).
- Synthesis: Oversight is distributed without fragmenting authority. The sovereign tends the domain, the watcher inspects it, and delegated speech and succession keep rule operative.

#### Subchannel B. Populace, workers, and ordered following
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Common subjects and workers are gathered into a body that follows its directing authority.
- Active motifs: populace below the king (`quranic:root_000762:B007/m01`); gathered community or mixture (`quranic:root_000259:B002/m01`); laboring workers (`quranic:root_001167:B003/m01`); following a leader (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`); many groups assembled (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29-30 `ٱلسَّاقُ`, `ٱلْمَسَاقُ` (س و ق); 75:17 `جَمْعَهُۥ` (ج م ع); 75:25 `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ع ل); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع); 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب).
- Synthesis: The directed collective has internal roles rather than anonymous mass: laborers act, subjects occupy political rank, and all movement is ordered by following.

### 24. P2: Rotating Lift and Carried Load
- Semantic invariant: Motion is transferred through a machine or harness to gather, lift, contain, and transport a load.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:16 anchors movement, 75:17 gathering, and 75:29-30 conveyance.
- Surprising reach: Controlled recitation and terminal driving both acquire the mechanics of hoist, wheel, waterskin, stirrup, and load-bearing back.

#### Subchannel A. Water-lifting apparatus
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A rotating implement raises water into a container and gathers the supply for use.
- Active motifs: wheel or lifting machine for water (`quranic:root_000987:B003/m01`); movement and turning (`quranic:root_000312:B001/m01`); large storage jar (`quranic:root_000286:B007/m01`); wetness and water held in a skin (`quranic:root_000152:B001/m01`); water gathered into one supply (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:16 `تُحَرِّكْ` (ح ر ك); 75:20 `تُحِبُّونَ` (ح ب ب); 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: Gathering depends on transmitted movement: rotation lifts dispersed water and deposits it in a stable vessel, a concrete analogue for controlled collection.

#### Subchannel B. Harnessed transport
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A rider takes a shortened route using a light container, stirrup gear, and an available animal back.
- Active motifs: light portable waterskin (`quranic:root_000987:B004/m01`); shortened route and accelerated travel (`quranic:root_000987:B008/m01`); stirrup strap (`quranic:root_000762:B009/m01`); loan of an animal's back for riding (`quranic:root_001169:B007/m01`); reaching the endpoint (`quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:29-30 `ٱلسَّاقُ`, `ٱلْمَسَاقُ` (س و ق); 75:25 `فَاقِرَةٌۭ` (ف ق ر); 75:26 `بَلَغَتِ` (ب ل غ).
- Synthesis: Speed is supported by equipment and borrowed carrying capacity. The route is shortened, but the traveler remains dependent on the harness that conveys him.

### 25. P2: A Following Herd That Splinters
- Semantic invariant: Young or dependent animals follow a mother and a driver until labor, loss, or dispersal breaks the herd into separated units.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:18 anchors following, 75:28 separation, and 75:29-30 driving.
- Surprising reach: Disciplined following and final conveyance are reframed through herd attachment, maternal dependence, and the vulnerability of stragglers.

#### Subchannel A. Calf following mother under a driver
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A calf remains attached to its mother while the herd is driven in sequence.
- Active motifs: calf and mother that it follows (`quranic:root_000175:B007/m01`); young calf (`quranic:root_000987:B002/m01`); herd-driving (`quranic:root_000762:B001/m01`); following in one trace (`quranic:root_000762:B012/m01`); following and catching the leader (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع); 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:29-30 `ٱلسَّاقُ`, `ٱلْمَسَاقُ` (س و ق).
- Synthesis: Following is an attachment relation before it is a route. The young animal's safety depends on maintaining both maternal and processional position.

#### Subchannel B. Flock divided by labor or loss
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A mixed herd breaks into a separated flock, a laboring female leaves the group, and displaced animals form a new mixture.
- Active motifs: herd or animal group (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); separated flock (`quranic:root_001148:B005/m01`); laboring female leaving the herd (`quranic:root_001148:B008/m01`); bereaved mother separated from offspring (`quranic:root_001148:B008/m02`); mixed outsiders absorbed into one body (`quranic:root_001365:B003/m01`); assembled animal or human mixture (`quranic:root_000259:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:23,30 `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف); 75:17 `جَمْعَهُۥ` (ج م ع).
- Synthesis: Separation reorganizes attachment. Labor and loss pull individuals out of the herd, while the remaining or displaced animals gather under a different collective identity.

### 26. P3: Truth Proven or Betrayed in Action
- Semantic invariant: Truth requires correspondence among inward commitment, spoken claim, and completed action; falsehood appears when any of these relations fail.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:31-32 oppose affirmation and prayer to denial and turning away.
- Surprising reach: Truth and lie are tested materially through solidity, fulfilled promise, sustained charge, and appearances that do not endure.

#### Subchannel A. Speech corresponding to its object
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A statement matches both the speaker's inward state and the reality it reports, while denial severs those correspondences.
- Active motifs: truthful speech matching mind and object (`quranic:root_000852:B001/m01`); speech or action contrary to truth (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); assigning falsehood to a speaker (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); lying self (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); inner nature or character (`quranic:root_000434:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق).
- Synthesis: The speech act is triadic: word, interior, and reported object must align. Denial is therefore not only an opposite assertion but a break in the speaker's own coherence.

#### Subchannel B. Promise verified by performance
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim is verified when its bearer completes the promised act and does not halt under pressure.
- Active motifs: promise or vision fulfilled in action (`quranic:root_000852:B004/m01`); charge that advances or fails in courage (`quranic:root_001290:B004/m01`); action performed without delay (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); undertaking begun and maintained (`quranic:root_000248:B004/m01`); undertaking entered as attachment (`quranic:root_001039:B016/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:39 `جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق).
- Synthesis: Truth becomes durative action. The claim is proven only when the participant enters the undertaking, remains attached to it, and carries it through the point where a false charge would stop.

#### Subchannel C. Solidity against deceptive appearance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A straight, solid object is distinguished from a colored surface whose appearance misrepresents its state.
- Active motifs: solidity and straightness (`quranic:root_000852:B002/m01`); complete and established goodness (`quranic:root_000852:B003/m01`); patterned cloth that lies by its appearance (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); gilding or deceptive gold coating (`quranic:root_000522:B002/m01`); straight and complete form (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:33 `ذَهَبَ` (ذ ه ب); 75:38 `سَوَّىٰ` (س و ي).
- Synthesis: Truth has structural depth; falsehood can be only a surface treatment. Straightness and solidity withstand inspection, while patterned cloth and gilding borrow value from appearance.

### 27. P3: Prayer as Approach and Close Following
- Semantic invariant: Prayer brings a participant into ordered proximity through bodily worship, blessing, and a following position behind what leads.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:31 names prayer, while 75:32 turns that approach into withdrawal.
- Surprising reach: Prayer is joined to the race-position of the horse immediately behind the leader and to the summons that calls bodies into worship.

#### Subchannel A. Prescribed worship and its place
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A worshipper answers a summons, enters the appointed place, and performs the bounded sequence of standing, bowing, and prostration.
- Active motifs: prescribed ritual prayer (`quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`); places set apart for prayer (`quranic:root_000879:B007/m01`); summons to come to prayer (`quranic:root_000383:B009/m01`); upright bodily form (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَلَّىٰ` (ص ل و); 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ` (ح ي ي); 75:38 `سَوَّىٰ` (س و ي).
- Synthesis: Prayer is an ordered bodily approach: call, place, stance, and sequence give devotion a concrete form rather than leaving it as inward assent.

#### Subchannel B. Blessing, mercy, and greeting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One participant addresses another with blessing, mercy, and a wish for enduring life and peace.
- Active motifs: prayer as blessing, praise, and mercy (`quranic:root_000879:B002/m01`); greeting as prayer for life and peace (`quranic:root_000383:B007/m01`); welcoming salutation that grants ease and belonging (`quranic:root_000064:B005/m01`); honorable remembrance and praise (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَلَّىٰ` (ص ل و); 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ` (ح ي ي); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:39 `ٱلذَّكَرَ` (ذ ك ر).
- Synthesis: Prayer extends from ritual performance into a life-giving social utterance. Blessing establishes relation by wishing survival, peace, and honored remembrance upon another.

#### Subchannel C. Staying immediately behind the leader
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A runner occupies the position just behind the leader and maintains uninterrupted proximity rather than turning out of the course.
- Active motifs: horse running second at the leader's flank (`quranic:root_000879:B006/m01`); nearness without interval (`quranic:root_001684:B001/m01`); succession without a break (`quranic:root_001684:B002/m01`); turning away and withdrawing (`quranic:root_001684:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَلَّىٰ` (ص ل و); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي).
- Synthesis: The race image gives prayer a relational position: successful approach means remaining close behind the lead; refusal is a turn out of that ordered proximity.

### 28. P3: Turning Away While Consequence Draws Near
- Semantic invariant: Bodily and relational orientation determines whether one approaches a rightful goal or turns away even as that goal becomes nearer and more commanding.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:32-35 move from turning away and departing home to the repeated `أَوْلَىٰ`.
- Surprising reach: Nearness functions as spatial proximity, superior claim, and arrival at a finish line, while swagger dramatizes misdirected bodily confidence.

#### Subchannel A. Face turned away into departure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A participant redirects face and attention away from a claim, then departs along another path.
- Active motifs: turning face or attention toward a direction (`quranic:root_001684:B006/m01`); turning the back and refusing engagement (`quranic:root_001684:B007/m01`); going away and passing (`quranic:root_000522:B006/m01`); path or manner of going (`quranic:root_000522:B007/m01`); terminal direction (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:33 `ذَهَبَ` (ذ ه ب); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: Refusal is directional. Attention turns, the body follows, and departure takes a route whose apparent divergence cannot remove the terminal goal.

#### Subchannel B. Nearness becomes superior claim
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What is spatially near acquires the stronger entitlement and closes upon the person as the reached limit.
- Active motifs: nearness without gap (`quranic:root_001684:B001/m01`); greater right and entitlement (`quranic:root_001684:B008/m01`); mastery and arrival at the goal (`quranic:root_001684:B012/m01`); fitness and rightful qualification (`quranic:root_000064:B003/m01`); terminal limit (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: The repeated threat is pressured by a claim that is both closer and more entitled. Consequence does not chase from afar; it already possesses the superior relation to the subject.

#### Subchannel C. Swaggered extension on the wrong route
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The departing person lengthens body and stride into a self-displaying gait on the road home.
- Active motifs: bodily extension (`quranic:root_001432:B001/m01`); prolonged travel (`quranic:root_001432:B002/m01`); swagger through stretched posture (`quranic:root_001432:B006/m01`); going away (`quranic:root_000522:B006/m01`); household destination (`quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `ذَهَبَ`, `أَهْلِهِۦ`, `يَتَمَطَّىٰ` (ذ ه ب، ء ه ل، م ط و).
- Synthesis: Swagger converts ordinary return into embodied defiance. The route leads home, but the elongated posture makes the traveler advertise command over a movement whose true endpoint he does not control.

### 29. P3: Claimed Status and Manufactured Display
- Semantic invariant: Social standing may rest on proven character and fitness, or be manufactured through pedigree, boastful posture, coating, and deceptive ornament.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:33-36 anchor swagger, repeated claims of priority, and the human being's supposition.
- Surprising reach: Reputation is tested like a crafted surface: true worth is load-bearing, while paint, cloth, and inherited claim can imitate it.

#### Subchannel A. Proven standing versus inherited boast
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person claims status from lineage or reputation, but genuine standing requires complete character and demonstrated fitness.
- Active motifs: pedigree, inherited merit, and boast (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`); honorable reputation (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); established excellence (`quranic:root_000852:B003/m01`); fitness for a role (`quranic:root_000064:B003/m01`); character and inner disposition (`quranic:root_000434:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب); 75:39 `ٱلذَّكَرَ` (ذ ك ر); 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق).
- Synthesis: Standing is not inherited inventory alone. Reputation and pedigree become valid only when inner character and proven qualification give them structural support.

#### Subchannel B. Gilded and perfumed appearance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person fabricates prestige by coating, coloring, patterning, and scenting an exterior.
- Active motifs: gilded coating (`quranic:root_000522:B002/m01`); cloth whose pattern misrepresents its state (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); perfumed coating (`quranic:root_000434:B010/m01`); women's perfume staining garments (`quranic:root_000058:B007/m01`); colored complexion from red toward yellow (`quranic:root_000522:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `ذَهَبَ` (ذ ه ب); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:39 `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` (ء ن ث).
- Synthesis: Display is assembled layer by layer. Gold wash, pattern, color, and scent can make an exterior socially persuasive without changing the material beneath it.

### 30. P3: Household Belonging and Contracted Kinship
- Semantic invariant: Belonging is produced through dwelling, welcome, marriage, alliance, and intimate companionship rather than mere physical return to a house.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:33 names the household, and 75:39 names the male-female pair.
- Surprising reach: The home scene unfolds into legal and affective relations: spouse, dowry, freed kin, ally, neighbor, and chosen companion.

#### Subchannel A. An inhabited and welcoming household
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Close relations share a dwelling that converts strangeness into familiarity and reception.
- Active motifs: household, spouse, and intimate group (`quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`); inhabited place and familiarity (`quranic:root_000064:B004/m01`); welcoming ease among one's own (`quranic:root_000064:B005/m01`); companionship that removes estrangement (`quranic:root_000059:B003/m01`); intimate companion or confidant (`quranic:root_000059:B006/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:36 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` (ء ن س).
- Synthesis: A house becomes `أهل` through relations of recognition. Dwelling, welcome, and conversation turn physical co-presence into belonging.

#### Subchannel B. Marriage, pairing, and dowry
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Two parties become household kin through pairing, marriage, and a legally recognized marital gift.
- Active motifs: taking a spouse and forming a household (`quranic:root_000064:B002/m01`); marital spouse (`quranic:root_000652:B002/m01`); making one party the mate of another (`quranic:root_000652:B003/m01`); woman's dowry (`quranic:root_000852:B007/m01`); kinship, marriage, and manumission bond (`quranic:root_001684:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:39 `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ` (ز و ج); 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي).
- Synthesis: Pairing becomes a social institution through household formation, gift, and recognized bond. Biological duality is thus embedded in legal relation.

#### Subchannel C. Friendship and protective alliance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Companions establish a relation of sincere affection, mutual defense, and shared presence.
- Active motifs: truthful friendship (`quranic:root_000852:B005/m01`); love, aid, and alliance (`quranic:root_001684:B004/m01`); companion and counterpart (`quranic:root_001432:B004/m01`); intimate companionship (`quranic:root_000059:B003/m01`); close household affiliation (`quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:33 `يَتَمَطَّىٰ`, `أَهْلِهِۦ` (م ط و، ء ه ل); 75:36 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` (ء ن س).
- Synthesis: Belonging also exists outside descent. Sincere attachment, protection, and shared movement create a chosen social bond that can support the household.

### 31. P3: What Is Left Requires a Weave
- Semantic invariant: Material left without active completion remains residue or bare warp; ordered joining converts it into a durable fabric.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:36 asks whether the human is to be left `سُدًى`.
- Surprising reach: Purposeless abandonment is materialized through orphaned remains, unattended pools and eggs, exposed warp, and cloth that wears out or deceives.

#### Subchannel A. Abandonment, residue, and inheritance
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An owner departs, leaving behind objects, traces, inheritance, and unattended natural remnants.
- Active motifs: releasing or abandoning a thing (`quranic:root_000180:B001/m01`); surviving trace left behind (`quranic:root_000180:B002/m01`); estate of the dead (`quranic:root_000180:B006/m01`); abandoned egg (`quranic:root_000180:B008/m01`); pool or meadow passed over and left (`quranic:root_000180:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `يُتْرَكَ` (ت ر ك).
- Synthesis: Being left is not featureless freedom. It produces an ecology of ownerless traces whose condition records the departure that abandoned them.

#### Subchannel B. Bare warp awaiting construction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Longitudinal threads are laid out but do not become cloth until complementary material crosses and binds them.
- Active motifs: warp threads opposed to weft (`quranic:root_000690:B006/m01`); small garment hanging on the body (`quranic:root_001039:B015/m01`); paired covering laid over a litter (`quranic:root_000652:B006/m01`); joining one thing to its mate (`quranic:root_000652:B001/m01`); smooth completed surface (`quranic:root_000434:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `سُدًى` (س د ي); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً`, `خَلَقَ` (ع ل ق، خ ل ق); 75:39 `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ` (ز و ج).
- Synthesis: Bare warp is ordered potential, not finished fabric. Completion requires crossing relation and pairing, making `سُدًى` a precise image for structure left without its completing operation.

#### Subchannel C. Worn or deceptive cloth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A finished textile can decay through use or conceal decay behind color and pattern.
- Active motifs: cloth worn smooth and ragged (`quranic:root_000434:B009/m01`); patterned cloth that lies by appearance (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); gilded surface treatment (`quranic:root_000522:B002/m01`); perfume applied as a coating (`quranic:root_000434:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:33 `ذَهَبَ` (ذ ه ب).
- Synthesis: Even completed fabric can lose integrity or mask it. Wear exposes the dependence of appearance on construction, while ornament can conceal structural exhaustion.

### 32. P3: Creation as Measured Fabrication
- Semantic invariant: Making proceeds from calculation and template, through origination and transformation, to straightening and surface completion.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:37-40 anchor existence, creation, proportioning, making, and capacity.
- Surprising reach: Divine formation is rendered as exact workshop practice: measuring before cutting, setting a state, and finishing a surface to fit.

#### Subchannel A. Measure and template before making
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A maker calculates dimensions, compares a template, and fixes the intended limit before acting on material.
- Active motifs: estimating and measuring before cutting (`quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`); bounded quantity (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); planned measurement and preparation (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); decreed design (`quranic:root_001450:B001/m01`); standard weight or measure (`quranic:root_001450:B006/m01`); counting and calculation (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:40 `قَٰدِرٍ` (ق د ر); 75:37 `مَّنِىٍّ`, `يُمْنَىٰ` (م ن ي); 75:36 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب).
- Synthesis: Creation begins as determinate intention. Measure and template make the eventual form accountable to a design before material transformation begins.

#### Subchannel B. Origination and change of state
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Material comes into presence, is made, and is set into a new condition.
- Active motifs: origination and making (`quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`); bringing a thing into existence (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`); converting a thing into a state (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); occurrence and presence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); undertaking begun (`quranic:root_000248:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `كَانَ`, `خَلَقَ` (ك و ن، خ ل ق); 75:39 `جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل).
- Synthesis: Origination and transformation are distinguished operations. A thing first occurs, then making acts upon it, and `جعل` assigns the resulting entity a new configured state.

#### Subchannel C. Straightening and finishing the form
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The created object is brought to balanced shape, smooth surface, and durable structural alignment.
- Active motifs: completed and proportioned image (`quranic:root_000434:B003/m01`); smoothing and leveling a surface (`quranic:root_000434:B008/m01`); straight and complete form (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`); solidity and straightness (`quranic:root_000852:B002/m01`); fit with the proper measure (`quranic:root_001205:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `خَلَقَ`, `سَوَّىٰ` (خ ل ق، س و ي); 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:40 `قَٰدِرٍ` (ق د ر).
- Synthesis: Finishing is not cosmetic. Surface, proportion, and structural straightness converge so that the form can endure and perform as intended.

### 33. P3: From Fluid Deposit to Attached Body
- Semantic invariant: A measured liquid deposit gathers, adheres, thickens, and remains implanted until it becomes a formed body.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:37-38 move from a drop of emitted fluid to an attached clot, creation, and proportion.
- Surprising reach: The embryonic sequence is supplied with fluid dynamics, adhesion, blood consistency, and gestational fixation.

#### Subchannel A. Emitted liquid and flowing drop
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Reproductive fluid is emitted as a small clear drop that can flow, drip, and spread.
- Active motifs: seminal fluid from which form is measured (`quranic:root_001450:B002/m01`); clear water from drop to abundance, including semen (`quranic:root_001518:B002/m01`); dripping, flowing, and extended wetness (`quranic:root_001518:B003/m01`); measured generative design (`quranic:root_001450:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:37 `نُطْفَةً`, `مَّنِىٍّ`, `يُمْنَىٰ` (ن ط ف، م ن ي).
- Synthesis: The starting material is both minimal and dynamic. Its smallness does not imply inertness: flow carries a measured generative capacity into the next stage.

#### Subchannel B. Adhesion, thick blood, and implantation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The deposit catches on a surface, thickens into blood-like material, and becomes fixed as a pregnancy.
- Active motifs: catching and adhering (`quranic:root_001039:B001/m01`); thick clotted blood (`quranic:root_001039:B003/m01`); conception fixed in the womb (`quranic:root_001039:B009/m01`); reproductive testing interval (`quranic:root_001450:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق); 75:37 `مَّنِىٍّ`, `يُمْنَىٰ` (م ن ي).
- Synthesis: The change from drop to `علقة` is a change of relation as well as substance. Fluid becomes a body-in-process when it adheres, thickens, and remains fixed.

#### Subchannel C. Gestation carried to completed form
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Pregnancy extends to term, the embodied state comes into being, and creation finishes it in balanced form.
- Active motifs: pregnancy extended until completion (`quranic:root_001432:B007/m01`); state coming into temporal presence (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); origination of the body (`quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`); completed visible form (`quranic:root_000434:B003/m01`); balanced bodily completion (`quranic:root_000766:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `يَتَمَطَّىٰ` (م ط و); 75:37-38 `يَكُ`, `كَانَ` (ك و ن); 75:38 `خَلَقَ`, `سَوَّىٰ` (خ ل ق، س و ي).
- Synthesis: Gestation is duration directed toward form. Extension holds the developing entity in time until origination, visible structure, and proportion converge.

### 34. P3: Sexed Pairing and Reproductive Relation
- Semantic invariant: Male and female are distinguished as embodied forms, joined as complementary mates, and situated within sexual and marital relations.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:39 names the two mates, male and female.
- Surprising reach: The binary includes anatomical, qualitative, classificatory, marital, and generative senses without reducing one operation to another.

#### Subchannel A. Male and female embodied forms
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Two sexed bodies are differentiated by reproductive identity and anatomy.
- Active motifs: male in contrast to female (`quranic:root_000516:B001/m01`); female in contrast to male (`quranic:root_000058:B001/m01`); paired organs named `الأنثيان` (`quranic:root_000058:B003/m01`); concealed reproductive organ or womb (`quranic:root_000383:B011/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:39 `ٱلذَّكَرَ`, `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` (ذ ك ر، ء ن ث); 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ` (ح ي ي).
- Synthesis: The pair is embodied rather than grammatical abstraction. Sex identity, paired anatomy, and reproductive interior make distinction materially operative.

#### Subchannel B. Complement, contrast, and class
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Two items are placed as mates because they complement, oppose, resemble, or define a class.
- Active motifs: each of two joined counterparts (`quranic:root_000652:B001/m01`); type or class within a larger set (`quranic:root_000652:B004/m01`); similar companions sharing form or action (`quranic:root_000652:B005/m01`); masculine hardness and sharpness (`quranic:root_000516:B002/m01`); feminine softness or weakness (`quranic:root_000058:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:39 `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ`, `ٱلذَّكَرَ`, `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` (ز و ج، ذ ك ر، ء ن ث).
- Synthesis: Pairing can organize both difference and likeness. The gendered hardness-softness contrast is one lexical axis, while class and resemblance show that `زوج` is a relational structure broader than anatomy.

#### Subchannel C. Mating, marriage, and conception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Desire brings two animals or persons together, pairing becomes marriage, and union establishes pregnancy.
- Active motifs: marital spouse (`quranic:root_000652:B002/m01`); actively pairing one with another (`quranic:root_000652:B003/m01`); female seeking the male for mating (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); conception fixed in the womb (`quranic:root_001039:B009/m01`); sexual attachment and love (`quranic:root_001039:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:39 `جَعَلَ`, `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ` (ج ع ل، ز و ج); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق).
- Synthesis: The channel keeps three operations distinct but sequential: attraction initiates encounter, pairing establishes relation, and conception gives the relation a new embodied outcome.

### 35. P3: Life Returning to Barren Ground
- Semantic invariant: Life is a capacity that can disappear from bodies or land, return through water and growth, and be preserved against destruction.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:40 opposes death and revival.
- Surprising reach: Revival is ecological as well as biological: rain, fertile soil, sprouting, animal life, survival, and public benefit all instantiate renewed vitality.

#### Subchannel A. Animate force opposed to death
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A living creature possesses spirit and force; death removes both, and revival restores effective life.
- Active motifs: animate creature possessing spirit (`quranic:root_000383:B003/m01`); loss of life and force (`quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`); weakening or extinguishing force (`quranic:root_001454:B002/m01`); life as benefit and rescue (`quranic:root_000383:B013/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ`, `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (ح ي ي، م و ت).
- Synthesis: Revival restores more than motion. The branch contrast includes spirit, force, and the capacity to benefit or rescue others.

#### Subchannel B. Rain revives dead land
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Barren ground receives successive rain, gathers water, and becomes fertile with fresh growth.
- Active motifs: rain and vegetation giving land life (`quranic:root_000383:B002/m01`); uncultivated dead land (`quranic:root_001454:B003/m01`); rain following the first seasonal rain (`quranic:root_001684:B010/m01`); rainfall passing over the land (`quranic:root_000522:B005/m01`); fertile ground quick to grow (`quranic:root_000058:B004/m01`); rock hollow or well collecting rainwater (`quranic:root_000434:B011/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ`, `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (ح ي ي، م و ت); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:33 `ذَهَبَ` (ذ ه ب); 75:39 `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` (ء ن ث); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق).
- Synthesis: Revival is a distributed water system. Successive rain enters receptive ground, collects, and converts an unproductive surface into rapid growth.

#### Subchannel C. Preservation against outbreak and loss
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A community or herd faces mortality, offspring loss, and killing, while a protector preserves survivors and sustaining traces.
- Active motifs: sparing life and withholding killing (`quranic:root_000383:B006/m01`); mortality outbreak among people or livestock (`quranic:root_001454:B004/m01`); parent or animal losing offspring (`quranic:root_001454:B005/m01`); life as rescue from destruction (`quranic:root_000383:B013/m01`); surviving trace left behind (`quranic:root_000180:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ`, `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (ح ي ي، م و ت); 75:36 `يُتْرَكَ` (ت ر ك).
- Synthesis: Life can be maintained within mass loss. Preservation acts selectively against outbreak, protecting persons, offspring, and the traces through which a damaged group can continue.

### 36. P3: Testing the Boundary of Death
- Semantic invariant: Stillness may be actual death, temporary incapacity, feigned death, or an uncertain state requiring verification.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:40 asks about reviving the dead.
- Surprising reach: The lexical field refuses to treat motionlessness as self-authenticating; sleep, swoon, dullness, performance, and inspection remain distinct states.

#### Subchannel A. Sleep, swoon, and deadened awareness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body or mind loses movement and awareness without necessarily crossing into irreversible death.
- Active motifs: sleep, still wind, or worn-out object called dead (`quranic:root_001454:B012/m01`); fit, swoon, or seizure followed by recovery (`quranic:root_001454:B009/m01`); deadened understanding (`quranic:root_001454:B006/m01`); bad state of being (`quranic:root_001332:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (م و ت); 75:37-38 `يَكُ`, `كَانَ` (ك و ن).
- Synthesis: Apparent lifelessness spans reversible bodily and cognitive states. Stillness therefore requires diagnosis rather than immediate classification as death.

#### Subchannel B. Feigned death and inspected death
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One subject performs death or false humility, while an observer tests another body to determine whether life remains.
- Active motifs: feigned death or false submissiveness (`quranic:root_001454:B011/m01`); inspecting struck prey for death (`quranic:root_001454:B014/m01`); false statement or act (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); direct inspection and calculation (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (م و ت); 75:32 `كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 75:36 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب).
- Synthesis: Death can be falsely displayed, so the body becomes an object of examination. The distinction between performance and irreversible state must be established rather than assumed.

### 37. P3: Appointed Mortality and Effective Capacity
- Semantic invariant: Death adheres as a measured limit, while effective power is the capacity to act through that limit and produce renewed life.
- Surface relation: direct; 75:36 asks what the human supposes, and 75:40 asserts capacity to revive the dead.
- Surprising reach: Power is neither unbounded force nor abstraction; it is measured fitness to accomplish an exact outcome despite weakness, constraint, and decreed mortality.

#### Subchannel A. Mortality as a clinging appointment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A decreed death approaches its measure, attaches to the person, and becomes a realized state.
- Active motifs: appointed death (`quranic:root_001450:B003/m01`); calamity or death that clings to a person (`quranic:root_001039:B014/m01`); one realized condition of dying (`quranic:root_001454:B008/m01`); bounded term and quantity (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); decreed design (`quranic:root_001450:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:37 `مَّنِىٍّ`, `يُمْنَىٰ` (م ن ي); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق); 75:40 `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ`, `قَٰدِرٍ` (م و ت، ق د ر).
- Synthesis: Mortality is both temporal and relational. Its term is measured, but its arrival is experienced as something that catches and remains attached to the person.

#### Subchannel B. Capacity that reaches an outcome
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A capable actor possesses enough effective power to perform the exact act denied by the question.
- Active motifs: effective capability and dominion (`quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`); ability and energy for an undertaking (`quranic:root_000076:B011/m01`); negation emphatically rejected (`quranic:root_001390:B001/m01`); life restored as rescue and benefit (`quranic:root_000383:B013/m01`); attaining the goal (`quranic:root_001684:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `أَلَيْسَ`, `قَٰدِرٍ`, `يُحْۦِىَ` (ل ي س، ق د ر، ح ي ي); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); surface anchor unavailable for ء ل ي.
- Synthesis: The question joins negation to capability and then overturns the negation. Capacity is demonstrated by reaching the specified outcome: renewed life.

#### Subchannel C. Measured constraint versus weakness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A limited measure can still be fit for purpose, whereas weak judgment or soft material fails under the required load.
- Active motifs: restriction to a narrow measure (`quranic:root_001205:B004/m01`); form fitted to a middle measure (`quranic:root_001205:B006/m01`); weak judgment (`quranic:root_001390:B007/m01`); softness and weakness (`quranic:root_000058:B002/m01`); hardness and cutting strength (`quranic:root_000516:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `قَٰدِرٍ`, `أَلَيْسَ` (ق د ر، ل ي س); 75:39 `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ`, `ٱلذَّكَرَ` (ء ن ث، ذ ك ر).
- Synthesis: Limitation and incapacity are not synonyms. Exact constraint can produce fit, while weakness is failure to bear or execute the intended operation.

### 38. P3: Mounted Return and Extended Travel
- Semantic invariant: A traveler mounts a padded back, extends the journey along a path, and carries provisions or goods toward a household destination.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:33 anchors going home with extended gait, and 75:36-38 anchor sending forth, being left, and attachment.
- Surprising reach: Swaggering return is grounded in the material logistics of pad, mount, path, baggage animal, and sustained travel.

#### Subchannel A. Pad, saddle, and mounted back
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Layers of padding prepare an animal's back to carry a mounted traveler.
- Active motifs: saddle pad placed on a camel's back (`quranic:root_000766:B010/m01`); under-saddle cloth (`quranic:root_001684:B011/m01`); mounting an animal's back (`quranic:root_001432:B003/m01`); paired cover laid over a litter (`quranic:root_000652:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `سَوَّىٰ` (س و ي); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:33 `يَتَمَطَّىٰ` (م ط و); 75:39 `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ` (ز و ج).
- Synthesis: Mounted movement depends on layered interfaces. Pad, cloth, and cover distribute load so that the rider's extended posture can be carried.

#### Subchannel B. Long route with a baggage animal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler stretches the route, sends hand and herd forward, and entrusts supplies to an animal accompanying a provisioning party.
- Active motifs: prolonged travel (`quranic:root_001432:B002/m01`); extending hand or herd in movement (`quranic:root_000690:B002/m01`); route and manner of going (`quranic:root_000522:B007/m01`); baggage animal sent with a provisioning party (`quranic:root_001039:B013/m01`); household destination (`quranic:root_000064:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:33 `يَتَمَطَّىٰ`, `ذَهَبَ`, `أَهْلِهِۦ` (م ط و، ذ ه ب، ء ه ل); 75:36 `سُدًى` (س د ي); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق).
- Synthesis: Extended travel is a coordinated supply scene rather than solitary stride. Route, animal, and carried provisions sustain the return to the household.

### 39. P3: Rendered Benefit and Answerable Exchange
- Semantic invariant: A deed creates a benefit, wage, gift, or enforceable right whose value is assigned to another and remains answerable.
- Surface relation: indirect; 75:31 anchors truthful performance, 75:36 supposition, and 75:39 making or assignment.
- Surprising reach: Moral action is expressed through alms, contractual wage, document of right, and a benefit deliberately rendered to another.

#### Subchannel A. Good rendered as gift, wage, or alms
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A benefactor performs a benefit and assigns it to another as gift, wage, or charity.
- Active motifs: rendering a good deed (`quranic:root_000690:B005/m01`); assigning good or harm to another (`quranic:root_001684:B013/m01`); wage set for completed work (`quranic:root_000248:B005/m01`); alms or obligatory monetary right (`quranic:root_000852:B006/m01`); life as public benefit (`quranic:root_000383:B013/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `سُدًى` (س د ي); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي); 75:39 `جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل); 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ` (ح ي ي).
- Synthesis: Benefit is directional action. It is made, valued, and placed with another person, so generosity remains a concrete transfer rather than an unlocated virtue.

#### Subchannel B. Documented right and practical accounting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A right is written, assessed, and collected as an accountable obligation.
- Active motifs: legal document recording a right (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); practical inspection and accounting (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`); alms as a due monetary right (`quranic:root_000852:B006/m01`); measured planning and calculation (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); entitlement and superior claim (`quranic:root_001684:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:39 `ٱلذَّكَرَ` (ذ ك ر); 75:36 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب); 75:31 `صَدَّقَ` (ص د ق); 75:40 `قَٰدِرٍ` (ق د ر); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي).
- Synthesis: Exchange becomes answerable when entitlement is documented and measured. The record keeps the right active until practical accounting completes the transfer.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1: Lots, Wagers, and the Allotted Share
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Players draw marked lots from a container, gamble for apportioned shares, and establish a winner.
- Active motifs: allotted share (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); gambling and victory by play or deception (`quranic:root_001255:B007/m01`); leather vessel holding lot-arrows (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); fifth gaming arrow (`quranic:root_001533:B016/m01`); victory over another (`quranic:root_001643:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر); 75:12 `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر).
- Synthesis: Apportionment here is determined under uncertainty. Container, marked pieces, wager, and victory form a complete game scene that pressures the pericope's questions of assigned outcome and attempted evasion.

### S2. P1: Noonday Heat and Thunderous Threat
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Heat reaches its standing midpoint while thunder-like warning announces a severe event.
- Active motifs: scorching midday (`quranic:root_001226:B002/m01`); sun standing at noon (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); first or fiercest heat (`quranic:root_001142:B007/m01`); star-rise associated with intensified heat (`quranic:root_000995:B014/m01`); lightning-and-thunder threat (`quranic:root_000108:B003/m01`); event-day and its severity (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:1,6 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م), `يَوْمِ`, `يَوْمُ` (ي و م); 75:10 `ٱلْمَفَرُّ` (ف ر ر); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); 75:7 `بَرِقَ` (ب ر ق).
- Synthesis: Severity is given a temporal and acoustic peak: the sun stops overhead, heat intensifies, and a flashing threat makes the event impossible to treat as distant weather.

### S3. P1: Sinking Terrain and the Road to a Ridge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler crosses smooth ground and pale stone, encounters subsiding soil, and follows a clear route toward a defensive ridge.
- Active motifs: broad smooth expanse (`quranic:root_000766:B009/m01`); flat basin or low ground (`quranic:root_001215:B006/m01`); ground swallowing what enters (`quranic:root_000410:B001/m01`); pale friable stone (`quranic:root_000121:B007/m01`); clear road (`quranic:root_001464:B007/m01`); mountain refuge (`quranic:root_001643:B001/m01`); leading mountain ridge (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:4 `نُّسَوِّىَ` (س و ي); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:8 `خَسَفَ` (خ س ف); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟`, `قَدَّمَ` (ن ب ء، ق د م); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر).
- Synthesis: The escape landscape is physically deceptive: open smoothness gives way to swallowing ground, while the visible road and ridge cannot guarantee refuge.

### S4. P1: Hidden Voice Becoming Echo and Public Speech
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A faint ambiguous sound gains articulation, is repeated by a resonant chamber, and becomes abundant public speech.
- Active motifs: faint indistinct sound (`quranic:root_001464:B006/m01`); gurgling or returning echo (`quranic:root_001215:B011/m01`); cries and water-like sounds (`quranic:root_000152:B010/m01`); articulated saying (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); person prolific in speech (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:10 `يَقُولُ` (ق و ل); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: The acoustic process moves from uncertain signal to socially circulating utterance. Resonance amplifies, but articulation determines whether the repeated sound becomes reportable speech.

### S5. P1: Self-Reproach Carried as Weight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The self recognizes fault, blames itself, and experiences the accumulated defect as a load it must bear.
- Active motifs: blame for a deserved fault (`quranic:root_001386:B001/m01`); burden, weight, and sin (`quranic:root_001643:B002/m01`); pride, resolve, or force of character within the self (`quranic:root_001533:B014/m01`); defects and sins accumulated in a person (`quranic:root_000995:B018/m01`); practical self-accounting (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:2 `ٱللَّوَّامَةِ` (ل و م); 75:11 `وَزَرَ` (و ز ر); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); 75:3 `يَحْسَبُ` (ح س ب).
- Synthesis: Reproach has mass. Recognition converts flaw into a carried obligation, and the self's strength is measured by whether it submits that weight to accounting or converts it into pride.

### S6. P1: The Grave-Tethered Mount
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A mount is restrained at its owner's grave, left without food or water, and brought to death beside the settled body.
- Active motifs: grave-tethered animal left to die (`quranic:root_000154:B006/m01`); lying supine (`quranic:root_001372:B009/m01`); fixed settlement (`quranic:root_001215:B003/m01`); saddle pad on a camel's back (`quranic:root_000766:B010/m01`); women's or herdsmen's mount (`quranic:root_001142:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:15 `أَلْقَىٰ` (ل ق ي); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:4 `نُّسَوِّىَ` (س و ي); 75:10 `ٱلْمَفَرُّ` (ف ر ر); surface anchor unavailable for ب ل ي.
- Synthesis: The burial custom stages failed mobility with precision: riding equipment remains, but restraint converts the mount from means of travel into a companion of the grave.

### S7. P1: Moonlit Hunting Without a Watchman
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter and nocturnal animals move under moonlight while livestock left without a guard become exposed.
- Active motifs: hunting by moonlight (`quranic:root_001255:B003/m01`); wakefulness under the moon (`quranic:root_001255:B009/m01`); livestock left unattended at night (`quranic:root_001255:B011/m01`); young wild animals (`quranic:root_001142:B004/m01`); small bird (`quranic:root_001142:B011/m01`); turtledove-like bird (`quranic:root_001255:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر); 75:10 `ٱلْمَفَرُّ` (ف ر ر).
- Synthesis: Moonlight organizes a nocturnal risk system of hunter, prey, wakefulness, and absent guard. The moon's loss removes both visibility and the rhythm by which the scene is watched.

### S8. P2: Grammatical Joining and Differentiation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A textual analyst joins letters, separates semantic faces, identifies intermediate sound, and assigns grammatical roles.
- Active motifs: letters joined within one word (`quranic:root_001365:B008/m01`); differentiated senses or textual faces (`quranic:root_001148:B015/m01`); intermediate articulation between two sounds (`quranic:root_000170:B011/m01`); grammatical objects (`quranic:root_001167:B007/m01`); compositional path or pattern (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m01`); poetic template (`quranic:root_001211:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:29 `ٱلْتَفَّتِ` (ل ف ف); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق); 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن); 75:25 `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ع ل); 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ` (ق ر ء).
- Synthesis: Textual coherence requires both combination and discrimination. Letters join locally, while phonetic, semantic, metrical, and grammatical boundaries keep the composition intelligible.

### S9. P2: Suspicion Manufactured into Accusation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Weak suspicion hardens into hostile allegation through fabricated speech and a two-faced presentation.
- Active motifs: accusation and suspect status (`quranic:root_000969:B004/m01`); habitual bad suspicion of others (`quranic:root_000969:B005/m01`); hostile accused party (`quranic:root_000969:B007/m01`); attributing unspoken falsehood to another (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); fabricated false account (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); double-faced exterior (`quranic:root_001630:B015/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:25,28 `تَظُنُّ`, `ظَنَّ` (ظ ن ن); 75:27 `قِيلَ` (ق و ل); 75:25 `يُفْعَلَ` (ف ع ل); 75:22,24 `وُجُوهٌ` (و ج ه).
- Synthesis: Suspicion becomes socially dangerous through production: allegation supplies a target, fabricated speech supplies content, and a divided face manages the accuser's public appearance.

### S10. P2: The Reader as Devotee and Student
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A disciplined reader receives a text, studies it with others, follows its wording, and turns reading into devotional understanding.
- Active motifs: reader as ascetic and learned devotee (`quranic:root_001210:B006/m01`); collective reading and study (`quranic:root_001211:B001/m01`); following a leading model (`quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`); meaning disclosed through eloquent speech (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`); linguistic fluency and proof (`quranic:root_001355:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:17-18 `قُرْءَانَهُۥ`, `قَرَأْنَٰهُ` (ق ر ء); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع); 75:19 `بَيَانَهُۥ` (ب ي ن); 75:16 `لِسَانَكَ` (ل س ن).
- Synthesis: Reading is a cultivated role. Reception, collaborative study, following, and comprehension bind textual competence to devotional formation.

### S11. P2: Borrowed Calf and Bereaved Mother
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A mother that lost its young is induced to yield milk by a borrowed calf, while maternal following and separation remain visible.
- Active motifs: bereaved camel or woman yearning for lost offspring (`quranic:root_000987:B006/m01`); borrowed calf used to bring down milk (`quranic:root_001355:B007/m01`); calf following its mother (`quranic:root_000175:B007/m01`); bereaved mother separated from offspring (`quranic:root_001148:B008/m02`); young calf (`quranic:root_000987:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:16,20 `لِتَعْجَلَ`, `ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ` (ع ج ل); 75:16 `لِسَانَكَ` (ل س ن); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع); 75:28 `ٱلْفِرَاقُ` (ف ر ق).
- Synthesis: The scene uses substitution to reactivate a broken maternal process. Following, loss, and induced milk show how bodily attachment can persist after the original relation has been severed.

### S12. P3: Trap, Struck Prey, and Death Inspection
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter sets a trap, catches prey, strikes it, checks for death, and distinguishes lawful carcass from unverified death.
- Active motifs: hunting snare (`quranic:root_000879:B004/m01`); prey caught in a trap (`quranic:root_001039:B011/m01`); inspection to verify prey's death (`quranic:root_001454:B014/m01`); carrion dead without proper slaughter (`quranic:root_001454:B007/m01`); courage that does not abandon an opponent (`quranic:root_001390:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَلَّىٰ` (ص ل و); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق); 75:40 `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ`, `أَلَيْسَ` (م و ت، ل ي س).
- Synthesis: Death is a legally and physically inspected outcome. Capture and impact do not suffice; the hunter must determine the body's state before classifying what has been taken.

### S13. P3: Woman Suspended Outside Marriage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A woman is neither released nor supported within marriage, leaving her between household statuses and recognized kinship bonds.
- Active motifs: woman left unmarried in her parents' house (`quranic:root_000180:B007/m01`); wife suspended without support or divorce (`quranic:root_001039:B008/m01`); marital spouse (`quranic:root_000652:B002/m01`); household formed by marriage (`quranic:root_000064:B002/m01`); legal kinship and marriage bond (`quranic:root_001684:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `يُتْرَكَ` (ت ر ك); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق); 75:39 `ٱلزَّوْجَيْنِ` (ز و ج); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:32,34-35 `تَوَلَّىٰ`, `أَوْلَىٰ` (و ل ي).
- Synthesis: Suspension is a distinct social operation: attachment remains legally active but fails to provide the household position that should follow from it.

### S14. P3: Courage, Self-Surrender, and Submission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person faces mortal danger without retreat, yields the self to the undertaking, and distinguishes true submission from passive weakness.
- Active motifs: courage that remains before an opponent (`quranic:root_001390:B004/m01`); self-surrender without concern for death (`quranic:root_001454:B010/m01`); submission to truth (`quranic:root_001454:B013/m01`); yielding through submission (`quranic:root_001332:B004/m01`); patient endurance and forbearance (`quranic:root_001390:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `أَلَيْسَ`, `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (ل ي س، م و ت); 75:37-38 `يَكُ`, `كَانَ` (ك و ن).
- Synthesis: Submission is not simple loss of force. Courage, endurance, and voluntary self-yielding distinguish committed surrender from incapacity or theatrical humility.

### S15. P3: Humanity as Inhabited Kinship
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Human presence transforms wilderness into inhabited space organized by household, clan, companionship, and peoplehood.
- Active motifs: human community opposed to wildness (`quranic:root_000059:B001/m01`); companionship removing estrangement (`quranic:root_000059:B003/m01`); inhabited and familiar place (`quranic:root_000064:B004/m01`); clan or descent-group (`quranic:root_000383:B010/m01`); distinct people or nation (`quranic:root_000180:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:36 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ`, `يُتْرَكَ` (ء ن س، ت ر ك); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل); 75:40 `يُحْۦِىَ` (ح ي ي).
- Synthesis: The human being is socially located rather than left as an isolated biological unit. Habitation, kin group, and companionship make life recognizably human.

### S16. P3: Corrective Particles Against Fabricated Speech
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A textual analyst uses negation, correction, gender marking, and explicit mention to distinguish a formed statement from fabricated discourse.
- Active motifs: corrective `لكن` returning from a prior claim (`quranic:root_001373:B002/m01`); emphatic negation with `ليس` (`quranic:root_001390:B001/m01`); grammatical feminization (`quranic:root_000058:B005/m01`); explicit mention in speech (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); fabricated false speech (`quranic:root_000434:B007/m01`); invented report without reality (`quranic:root_001450:B013/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `أَلَيْسَ` (ل ي س); 75:39 `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ`, `ٱلذَّكَرَ` (ء ن ث، ذ ك ر); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:37 `مَّنِىٍّ`, `يُمْنَىٰ` (م ن ي); surface anchor unavailable for ل ك ن.
- Synthesis: Discourse is repaired through explicit operators. Correction and negation reopen a false construction, while grammatical marking and mention specify what the statement actually commits to.

### S17. P3: Pot, Rendered Fat, and Heat-Reduced Force
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A cook lowers a pot with a protective cloth, renders fat, and uses heat to reduce the strength of its contents.
- Active motifs: cooking pot and prepared stew (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`); cloth used to lower a hot pot (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`); rendered fat or oil used as food (`quranic:root_000064:B006/m01`); force weakened by cooking (`quranic:root_001454:B002/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:40 `قَٰدِرٍ`, `ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ` (ق د ر، م و ت); 75:39 `جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل); 75:33 `أَهْلِهِۦ` (ء ه ل).
- Synthesis: Heat performs controlled transformation: protective handling positions the vessel, fat changes state, and cooking deliberately subdues raw force.

### S18. Whole Surah: Grinding and Applying Perfume
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A perfumer pulverizes aromatics on a broad stone, compounds a women's perfume, applies it as a coating, and leaves a fragrance that adheres to its wearer or garment.
- Active motifs: broad grinding stone used for perfume (`quranic:root_000879:B008/m01`); women's perfume that colors garments (`quranic:root_000058:B007/m01`); perfume applied as a coating (`quranic:root_000434:B010/m01`); fragrance that clings to an object (`quranic:root_000155:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:31 `صَلَّىٰ` (ص ل و); 75:39 `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` (ء ن ث); 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:4 `بَنَانَهُۥ` (ب ن ن).
- Synthesis: Grinding, compounding, and coating deliberately manufacture an attractive exterior whose color and scent persist. This usefully pressures the surah's radiant and grim faces and its status display: a surface can be produced and made to cling, while 75:14 locates decisive witness in the self.

### S19. Whole Surah: Measured Tanning and a Stitched Waterskin
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A leatherworker measures hide before cutting, treats it with tanning foliage in small doses, folds it while wet, stitches the panels, and finishes or repairs the vessel with a thick coating.
- Active motifs: hide measured before cutting (`quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`); foliage and fruit used for tanning (`quranic:root_000076:B005/m01`); one or two small tanning doses (`quranic:root_001533:B007/m01`); waterskin folded while wet (`quranic:root_000152:B007/m01`); one hide stitched to another (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); thick coating used to repair hide or waterskin (`quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:38 `خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:12,23,30 `رَبِّكَ`, `رَبِّهَا`, `رَبِّكَ` (ر ب ب); surface anchors unavailable for ء ل ي and ب ل ل.
- Synthesis: The craft turns measured, treated, and joined skin into a vessel that can hold. This materializes the surah's bodily assembly through a human analogue of measured joining and repaired hide, while pressing the difference: a patched container is not living re-creation down to the fingertips.

### S20. Whole Surah: Circumcision and Its Feast
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A cutting implement performs a bodily rite, the excised tissue marks the operation, and a communal meal celebrates its completion.
- Active motifs: circumcision and the tissue cut away (`quranic:root_000995:B010/m01`); adze construed as a circumcision implement (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m02`); feast held for a circumcision (`quranic:root_000995:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); 75:13 `قَدَّمَ` (ق د م).
- Synthesis: The rite makes bodily alteration deliberate, witnessed, and socially marked. It materializes and pressures 75:3-4 and 75:37-40: human hands can remove tissue and commemorate the change, while the surah attributes exact forming and re-forming, including terminal bodily detail and sexed embodiment, to divine capacity.

### S21. Whole Surah: From Brain Wound to Blood-Oath Claim
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A crushing instrument opens a head wound, blood and bodily trace record the injury, and the victim's kin convert that trace into apportioned oaths and a claim for vengeance or compensation.
- Active motifs: stone or instrument crushing the head and reaching the brain (`quranic:root_000053:B003/m01`); penetrating wound approaching the brain or body cavity (`quranic:root_001518:B005/m01`); flowing blood as the bearer of life (`quranic:root_001533:B004/m01`); blood trace on body or ground linked to vengeance or compensation (`quranic:root_000121:B004/m01`); blood oaths divided among the victim's heirs (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); claimant pursuing vengeance, compensation, or another right (`quranic:root_000175:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:37 `نُطْفَةً` (ن ط ف); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س); 75:7,14 `ٱلْبَصَرُ`, `بَصِيرَةٌ` (ب ص ر); 75:1-2 `أُقْسِمُ` (ق س م); 75:18 `فَٱتَّبِعْ` (ت ب ع).
- Synthesis: The causal record passes from injured body to visible trace, sworn speech, and enforceable claim. This materializes 75:13-15: an act leaves evidence and liability that later words must answer, so an offered excuse cannot dissolve the embodied record.

### S22. Whole Surah: Archery Marks, Aim, Hit, and Miss
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An archer takes the inward-facing side of a bow, uses marked ground to define the shot, aims at the intended object, and produces either an exact hit or a deflection that lands elsewhere without a scratch.
- Active motifs: side of the bow facing the archer (`quranic:root_000059:B004/m01`); ground marks used for archery (`quranic:root_001169:B005/m01`); arrow or spear directed toward an intended target (`quranic:root_000053:B012/m01`); attaining the sought target (`quranic:root_000690:B008/m01`); arrow turning aside and failing to scratch (`quranic:root_001464:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3,5,10,13-14,36 `ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ` (ء ن س); 75:25 `فَاقِرَةٌ` (ف ق ر); 75:5 `أَمَامَهُۥ` (ء م م); 75:36 `سُدًى` (س د ي); 75:13 `يُنَبَّؤُا۟` (ن ب ء).
- Synthesis: The range separates intention, execution, and result: aiming forward does not guarantee that the arrow arrives. This materializes the surah's distinction between what a person intends or sends ahead and what is actually accomplished, while the exact hit reframes the effective capacity asserted in 75:40.

### S23. Whole Surah: Date Palms Arrested Before Sweetness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Seed-grown date stock rises as young palms, extends bunches of green fruit, and then suffers cold before ripening, leaving the dates without their expected sweetness.
- Active motifs: seed-grown date palms of unknown variety (`quranic:root_000259:B011/m01`); young or short palms (`quranic:root_000248:B006/m01`); extended palm bunch (`quranic:root_001432:B005/m01`); green dates hanging on loosened clusters (`quranic:root_000690:B003/m01`); fruit handled or present before ripeness (`quranic:root_000114:B001/m01`); dates struck by cold before maturity and stripped of sweetness (`quranic:root_001255:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:3,9,17 `نَّجْمَعَ`, `جُمِعَ`, `جَمْعَهُۥ` (ج م ع); 75:39 `جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل); 75:33 `يَتَمَطَّىٰ` (م ط و); 75:36 `سُدًى` (س د ي); 75:8-9 `ٱلْقَمَرُ` (ق م ر); 75:24 `بَاسِرَةٌ` (ب س ر).
- Synthesis: Visible bunches are not yet mature fruit, and an untimely cold can arrest the crop before sweetness forms. This materializes the immediate-versus-afterward contrast of 75:20-24 as an exact cultivation sequence and reframes the grim face as a ripening process fixed in a failed state.

### S24. Whole Surah: One Pour, Several Drinking Breaths
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An ewer pours life-sustaining water into a wooden cup in one movement, while the drinker receives it through measured sips and pauses for breath.
- Active motifs: shining ewer or pouring vessel (`quranic:root_000108:B006/m01`); water poured at once into a vessel (`quranic:root_001215:B005/m01`); wooden drinking cup (`quranic:root_001516:B003/m01`); drinking measured by sip, breath, and pause (`quranic:root_001533:B006/m01`); water sufficient to sustain and refresh life (`quranic:root_001533:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:7 `بَرِقَ` (ب ر ق); 75:12 `ٱلْمُسْتَقَرُّ` (ق ر ر); 75:22 `نَّاضِرَةٌ` (ن ض ر); 75:2,14 `ٱلنَّفْسِ`, `نَفْسِهِۦ` (ن ف س).
- Synthesis: The vessel can receive a complete pour while the body receives it by paced breaths. This materially reframes 75:16-19: collection may be assured and complete without the receiver rushing the tongue, and measured intake makes disciplined following bodily intelligible.

### S25. Whole Surah: Finger or Leech at the Uvula
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A practitioner confronts a throat disease near the uvula, raises or presses a child's affected tissue with a finger or applies a leech to draw blood, and invokes protective healing.
- Active motifs: throat disease at or near the uvula (`quranic:root_000995:B013/m01`); finger treatment that lifts or presses the affected tissue (`quranic:root_001039:B012/m01`); leech applied to draw blood (`quranic:root_001039:B012/m02`); protective incantation and healer (`quranic:root_000588:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 75:15 `مَعَاذِيرَهُۥ` (ع ذ ر); 75:38 `عَلَقَةً` (ع ل ق); 75:27 `رَاقٍ` (ر ق ي).
- Synthesis: The intervention gives throat illness a precise site, hand action, and blood-drawing instrument. This materializes 75:26-27 by placing a familiar treatment beside the terminal passage: ordinary healing can target a local lesion, but the ascent to the collarbones exceeds its bounded operation.

### S26. Whole Surah: When the Wind Dies, the Vessel Stalls
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A captain waits for the wind's proper time, but the wind falls dead and the vessel stops at sea despite the sailor's command.
